# HOUSE-RUNTIME-1b: recoverable D1362/D1373 authored source checkpoint

**Disposition: NOT_FULLY_QUALIFIED.** This owned evidence file preserves the actual
House claim-transition component, one-carrier House actor implementation, real native
materialization stage, tests, exact dependency patches and independent reviews. The
embedded patches are inert source backups. This checkpoint does not apply held shared
Rust hunks, open entry/login, or mark the PR ready. The existing runtime gate remains
`HouseClosed`; exit remains `ExitNotAdmitted`.

## Implemented behavior in the preserved source

### Claim transition and history

The additive House lifecycle shape compares the complete native operation, both full
guard predecessor rows and immutable history. Both holders move to the same destination
session, and the Character lease advances exactly once. The original Platform security
observation keeps its strict five-second age; it is never refreshed to make a stale
grant look current. The private preparation reads the locked PREPARED handoff, exact
source session, access/assignments/current node, the original initial receipt key and
the real #2163 interior loader in the caller's physical SQL transaction.

Persistence binds that same transaction, rechecks the actual durable unbound House
successor and exactly one real session-use ledger advance/membership, then rechecks
the complete guards/history before writing the canonical claim result. A tag-3 history
result requires Released; an old Committed handoff without the new receipt cannot be
promoted into one. Historical decoding/replay is data validation, not a way to recreate
live prepared authority. The tag is provisional until the allocated post-#2187 slot.

Both generation comparisons now use numeric ordering only within the same scope
identity. A legal initial Channel generation 5 to House generation 1 is covered through
the real persisted-facts constructor and a later TerminalRelease predicate; a rollback
within the original Channel still refuses. These are in-memory regression facts, not
PostgreSQL persistence evidence.

### One physical actor carrier

The existing carrier retains its actual arena/free list and gains a private Channel or
House scope domain and a HousePlayer slot arm. Existing Channel ActorRef APIs and the
Channel placement digest remain unchanged. The native HouseId implementation and byte
encoder have a single definition, moved into the owned module and re-exported at the
old public path; there is no synthetic ChannelId or second HouseId.

A House reservation occupies its actual slot and tile immediately. Every reservation
phase participates in House collision; materialization, consumption and a retained
unknown COMMIT use that same slot. Materialization copies the real source ActorState,
complete VersionedPosition, lifecycle/conditions and combat state under the actual
source hold. Missing position or a changed lease refuses; no default player is created.

Drop before commit releases the exact House slot and source fence, including panic
unwind. An unknown result retains both footprints. Reconciliation requires the exact
initial receipt, House/generation/node, source namespace and physical binding. Prepared
can perform denial-only rollback in the retained retired namespace without reopening
it. Released cannot remove a source from another Channel or discard an actual source
payload changed after the uncertain outcome. Exhausted arena generations retire the
unusable head and preserve access to later free slots. These are source-reviewed native
arena operations; final durable reconciliation must still be called by its actual owner.

### Actual native staged consumer

The owned stage consumes the real private native preparation, holds the actual borrowed
SQL transaction and both physical owner borrows, and verifies the physical transaction
identity, exact source session/lease/scope, actual runtime pin, canonical proposed tile,
qualified single-House membership/digest, and locked 0047 House identity/ownership. It
retains the original #2163 interior snapshot and validates its finite item positions
against that House's qualified cells before actual source hold, reservation,
materialization and consumption. A caller-proposed position is only a proposal; it
must equal the native locked PREPARED tile.

There is no bind or COMMIT method on this stage. It writes no successor session,
receipt, lease, transport or generation. The external PlayerSpellState owner has not
been transferred, and the loaded interior is a point-in-time snapshot. The code cannot
promote this partial materialization to gameplay. Unknown qualified House content
refuses before reservation.

## Recovery format

Every embedded artifact has its exact byte count and SHA-256. All implementation patches
are readable below; the broad native-format reproduction patch uses gzip/base64
to avoid obscuring the semantic changes. Native raw JSON/empty files without a terminal
newline also use gzip/base64, preserving their exact bytes. Where original patch context contains trailing
whitespace, `utf8-visible-whitespace` displays those bytes as `␠` (space) or `␉` (tab),
so the Markdown file itself has no trailing whitespace. Extraction restores the original
bytes before checking their hash. The following standard-library script extracts
the artifact blocks, checks every hash before writing, and rejects path traversal. It
does not fetch, execute code, apply a patch, start PostgreSQL or alter any checkout.
Use a new scratch directory for extraction and the profile instructions above to restore
the intended source variant.

```python
from pathlib import Path
import base64, gzip, hashlib, re, sys
document = Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text()
output = Path(sys.argv[2])
pattern = re.compile(
    r"<!-- BEGIN ARTIFACT (\S+) (utf8|utf8-visible-whitespace|gzip-base64) ([0-9a-f]{64}) ([0-9]+) -->\n"
    r"````[^\n]*\n(.*?)````\n<!-- END ARTIFACT \1 -->", re.S)
for match in pattern.finditer(document):
    name, encoding, expected, length, body = match.groups()
    relative = Path(name)
    if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts:
        raise ValueError(name)
    if encoding == "utf8-visible-whitespace":
        data = body.replace("␠", " ").replace("␉", "\t").encode()
    elif encoding == "utf8":
        data = body.encode()
    else:
        data = gzip.decompress(base64.b64decode(body, validate=False))
    if len(data) != int(length) or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != expected:
        raise ValueError("artifact integrity mismatch: " + name)
    target = output / relative
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_bytes(data)
    print(name, expected)
```

+// Historical-only decoder for the additive House lifecycle shape. The immutable
+// initial receipt is independently loaded by the adapter. None of these decoded
+// rows can construct the sealed HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1 preparation.
+fn decode_house_lifecycle(
+    encoded: &str,
+    initial: FreshAdmissionCommit<AuthenticatedTransportRefV1>,
+) -> Result<AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1> {
+    let bytes = decode_envelope(encoded, super::MAX_FRESH_OPERATION_BYTES)?;
+    let mut reader = Reader::new(&bytes);
+    // D1362 proposal: this tag must be re-cited on the post-#2187 base.
+    if reader.tag()? != 3 {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    let account_id = reader.text()?;
+    for expected in [
+        initial.game_session_id().as_bytes(),
+        initial.character_id().as_bytes(),
+        initial.world_id().as_bytes(),
+        initial.channel_id().as_bytes(),
+    ] {
+        if reader.bytes::<16>()? != *expected {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+        }
+    }
+    let current_id = checked(GameSessionId::decode(&reader.bytes::<16>()?))?;
+    if reader.u64()? != initial.character_lease_generation()
+        || reader.u64()? != initial.scope_ownership_generation()
+        || reader.u64()? != initial.connection_generation().get()
+        || reader.bytes::<16>()? != initial.initial_transport().to_bytes()
+    {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    let state = match reader.tag()? {
+        1 => GameSessionState::Active,
+        2 => GameSessionState::Reconnectable,
+        3 => GameSessionState::Terminal,
+        _ => return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState),
+    };
+    let connection = checked(ConnectionGeneration::new(reader.u64()?))?;
+    let transport = if reader.boolean()? {
+        Some(checked(AuthenticatedTransportRefV1::decode(&reader.bytes::<16>()?))?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    let lease = checked(CharacterLease::new(
+        checked(CharacterId::decode(&reader.bytes::<16>()?))?, reader.u64()?,
+    ))?;
+    let eligibility = if reader.boolean()? {
+        Some(CharacterWorldEligibilityClaimV1::new(
+            checked(CharacterId::decode(&reader.bytes::<16>()?))?,
+            checked(WorldId::decode(&reader.bytes::<16>()?))?,
+        ))
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    let scope = read_scope(&mut reader)?;
+    let generation = checked(ScopeOwnershipGeneration::new(reader.u64()?))?;
+    let epoch = if reader.boolean()? {
+        Some(checked(ControlLossEpochRefV1::new(reader.u64()?))?)
+    } else {
+        None
+    };
+    let grace = if reader.boolean()? { Some(reader.i64()?) } else { None };
+    // This is a historical source snapshot, validated as data. Current authority
+    // and native House permission must still be independently re-read by owners.
+    let mut current_session = checked(GameSessionAuthoritySnapshot::from_persisted_current_facts(
+        current_id, initial, state, connection, transport, lease, eligibility, scope, generation,
+    ))?;
+    match (epoch, grace) {
+        (None, None) => {}
+        (Some(epoch), Some(grace)) => {
+            current_session = checked(current_session.with_control_loss_continuity(epoch, grace))?;
+        }
+        _ => return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState),
+    }
+    let handoff_id = reader.bytes::<16>()?;
+    let destination_game_session_id = checked(GameSessionId::decode(&reader.bytes::<16>()?))?;
+    let destination_scope = read_scope(&mut reader)?;
+    let destination_scope_ownership_generation = reader.u64()?;
+    let destination_house_key = reader.text()?;
+    let tile_length = checked(usize::try_from(reader.u64()?))?;
+    if tile_length == 0 || tile_length > super::house_scope_handoff::HOUSE_TILE_MAX_BYTES {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    let reserved_entry_tile = reader.take(tile_length)?.to_vec();
+    let house = super::house_scope_handoff::HouseId::new(
+        initial.world_id(), &destination_house_key,
+    ).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    if house.runtime_scope()? != destination_scope {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    let evidence = AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1 {
+        operation: AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+            account_id, current_session, handoff_id, destination_game_session_id,
+            destination_scope, destination_scope_ownership_generation,
+            destination_house_key, reserved_entry_tile,
+        },
+        transition: AdmissionClaimTransitionEvidenceV1 {
+            predecessors: read_changes(&mut reader)?,
+            successors: read_changes(&mut reader)?,
+            prepared_at: reader.i64()?,
+        },
+    };
+    reader.finish()?;
+    // Retain the complete byte identity, including every optional source fence,
+    // claim predecessor/successor, order, length and timestamp.
+    if encode_lifecycle(&evidence)? != encoded {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    Ok(evidence)
 }

+// D1362: this adapter is an uninstalled post-#2187 component. Its real native
+// caller must place the House actor and consume the entry reservation before
+// applying the claim/bind/generation effects. No runtime caller is enabled here.
+struct NativeHouseClaimOwner {
+    operation: AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1,
+    transition: AdmissionClaimTransitionEvidenceV1,
+}
+impl fresh_source_sealed::Sealed for NativeHouseClaimOwner {}
+impl AdmissionClaimOwningSourceV1 for NativeHouseClaimOwner {
+    fn prepare_lifecycle_claim(
+        &self,
+        operation: &AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1,
+        _now: i64,
+    ) -> std::result::Result<AdmissionClaimLifecycleResolutionV1, AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1> {
+        if operation != &self.operation {
+            return Err(AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1::Stale);
+        }
+        Ok(AdmissionClaimLifecycleResolutionV1 {
+            current_session: self.operation.current_session(),
+            evidence: self.transition.clone(),
+        })
+    }
+    fn prepare_fresh_claim(
+        &self,
+        _binding: &FreshAdmissionAuditBindingV1,
+        _now: i64,
+    ) -> std::result::Result<AdmissionClaimTransitionEvidenceV1, AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1> {
+        Err(AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1::Unavailable)
+    }
+}
+
+/// Private same-transaction preparation, not a live placement capability and
+/// not constructible from historical receipt bytes or caller-supplied guards.
+pub(super) struct PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims {
+    context: super::house_scope_handoff::PreparedHouseClaimContext,
+    transition: HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1,
+    rows: Vec<Option<AdmissionAuthorityPublicationChangeV1>>,
+    encoded_successors: Vec<String>,
+    encoded: String,
+    initial_fresh_replay_key: Vec<u8>,
+    predecessor_session_use_revision: u64,
+    transaction_id: String,
+}
+impl PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims {
+    /// Point-in-time native materialization input for the eventual House owner.
+    /// It cannot stand in for actual actor placement or current runtime authority.
+    pub(super) fn interior(&self) -> &super::house_interior::HouseInteriorSnapshot {
+        self.context.interior()
+    }
+}
+
+impl FreshAdmissionStore {
+    pub(super) async fn prepare_house_scope_transition_claims(
+        &self,
+        tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+        node: &super::runtime_scope_assignment::NodeIncarnationProof,
+        commit: &super::house_scope_handoff::HouseEntryCommit,
+    ) -> std::result::Result<PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims, super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffError> {
+        use super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffError;
+        let context = super::house_scope_handoff::prepare_house_claim_context_in_transaction(
+            tx, node, commit,
+        ).await?;
+        // This lookup joins the actual immutable fresh receipt and validates
+        // the complete current session row; no expected DTO becomes authority.
+        let current = self.current_session_locked(tx, context.source_session()).await?;
+        let operation = context.operation(current)?;
+        let initial_fresh_replay_key: Option<Vec<u8>> = sqlx::query_scalar(
+            "SELECT COALESCE(fresh_replay_key,initial_fresh_replay_key) \
+             FROM game_durability_reconnect_sessions \
+             WHERE game_session_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+        )
+        .bind(current.current_game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .fetch_one(&mut **tx)
+        .await?;
+        let initial_fresh_replay_key = initial_fresh_replay_key.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+        checked(FreshAdmissionReplayKey::decode(&initial_fresh_replay_key))?;
+        let predecessor_session_use_revision = unused_session_revision(
+            tx, current.commit().character_id(), commit.destination_game_session_id,
+        ).await?;
+        let mut rows = Vec::with_capacity(2);
+        for key in [
+            AdmissionAuthorityGuardKeyV1::Account { account_id: operation.account_id().into() },
+            AdmissionAuthorityGuardKeyV1::Character(current.commit().character_id()),
+        ] {
+            // load_locked verifies both complete mirrors and the immutable
+            // latest publication history, under the actual owner transaction.
+            rows.push(self.guards.load_locked(tx, &key).await?);
+        }
+        let predecessors = rows.iter().cloned().collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()
+            .ok_or(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        let now: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT floor(extract(epoch FROM clock_timestamp()))::bigint")
+            .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+        if commit.committed_at > now {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::InvalidInput);
+        }
+        let mut successors = predecessors.clone();
+        for row in &mut successors {
+            if row.source.authority != super::fresh_admission_composition::COMPOSITION_SOURCE_AUTHORITY {
+                return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+            }
+            row.precondition = AdmissionPublicationPreconditionV1::CompareAndSet {
+                expected_publication_revision: row.publication_revision,
+            };
+            row.publication_revision = row.publication_revision.checked_add(1)
+                .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            row.source.source_revision = row.source.source_revision.checked_add(1)
+                .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+            row.source.decision_identity = format!("house-scope-entry:{}:{}", uuid_text(&commit.handoff_id), row.source.source_revision);
+            row.source.source_observed_at = now;
+            row.source.clock_uncertainty_seconds = 0;
+            match &mut row.state {
+                AdmissionAuthorityGuardStateV1::Account { security, presence } => {
+                    // Preserve the Platform observation, including its age.
+                    // Only its binding to this new Game publication changes.
+                    security.provenance.publication_revision = row.publication_revision;
+                    *presence = Some((current.commit().character_id(), commit.destination_game_session_id));
+                }
+                AdmissionAuthorityGuardStateV1::Character { lease_generation, holder, .. } => {
+                    *lease_generation = lease_generation.checked_add(1)
+                        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+                    *holder = Some(commit.destination_game_session_id);
+                }
+                _ => return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into()),
+            }
+        }
+        let source = NativeHouseClaimOwner {
+            operation: operation.clone(),
+            transition: AdmissionClaimTransitionEvidenceV1 { predecessors, successors, prepared_at: now },
+        };
+        let transition = HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1::prepare(&source, operation.clone(), now)
+            .map_err(|_| HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        transition.validate_locked(&operation, &rows, now)
+            .map_err(|_| HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        if !self.guards.successor_history_available(tx, &transition.evidence().transition.successors, &rows).await? {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+        }
+        let encoded_successors = transition.evidence().transition.successors.iter()
+            .map(|row| encode_guard(row, self.guards.maximum_guard_bytes))
+            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        let encoded = encode_lifecycle(transition.evidence())?;
+        let transaction_id = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT txid_current()::text")
+            .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+        Ok(PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims {
+            context, transition, rows, encoded_successors, encoded,
+            initial_fresh_replay_key, predecessor_session_use_revision, transaction_id,
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// Consume only a preparation from this exact physical transaction, after
+    /// the real native actor owner placed the actor and consumed its reservation.
+    /// This verifies the resulting durable shape; that shape is not itself an
+    /// actor-placement capability. Integration stays closed until that owner exists.
+    pub(super) async fn persist_house_scope_transition_claims(
+        &self,
+        tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+        prepared: PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims,
+    ) -> std::result::Result<GameSessionAuthoritySnapshot<AuthenticatedTransportRefV1>, super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffError> {
+        use super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffError;
+        let transaction_id: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT txid_current()::text")
+            .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+        if transaction_id != prepared.transaction_id {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+        }
+        prepared.context.verify_durable_successor(tx).await?;
+        let evidence = prepared.transition.evidence();
+        let AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+            current_session, destination_game_session_id, destination_scope,
+            destination_scope_ownership_generation, handoff_id, ..
+        } = &evidence.operation else {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+        };
+        verify_house_session_use_in_transaction(
+            tx, current_session.commit().character_id(), *destination_game_session_id,
+            *handoff_id, HouseSessionUseCheck::OwningSuccessor {
+                predecessor_revision: prepared.predecessor_session_use_revision,
+            },
+        ).await?;
+        let mut rows = Vec::with_capacity(2);
+        for prior in &evidence.transition.predecessors {
+            rows.push(self.guards.load_locked(tx, &prior.key).await?);
+        }
+        if rows != prepared.rows
+            || !self.guards.successor_history_available(tx, &evidence.transition.successors, &rows).await?
+        {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+        }
+        let decided_at: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT floor(extract(epoch FROM clock_timestamp()))::bigint")
+            .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+        // The sealed predicate already validated the independent current
+        // predecessor before the owning effects in this same locked TX. It is
+        // never reconstructed from the expected payload after terminalization.
+        evidence.validate_historical(decided_at)
+            .map_err(|_| HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+        self.guards.persist_locked(tx, &evidence.transition.successors, &rows, &prepared.encoded_successors).await?;
+        let inherited: Option<Vec<u8>> = sqlx::query_scalar(
+            "UPDATE game_durability_reconnect_sessions d \
+             SET initial_fresh_replay_key = (SELECT COALESCE(s.fresh_replay_key,s.initial_fresh_replay_key) \
+                 FROM game_durability_reconnect_sessions s \
+                 WHERE s.game_session_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid) \
+             WHERE d.game_session_id = encode($2,'hex')::uuid \
+               AND d.fresh_replay_key IS NULL AND d.initial_fresh_replay_key IS NULL \
+             RETURNING d.initial_fresh_replay_key",
+        )
+        .bind(current_session.current_game_session_id().as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(destination_game_session_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?.flatten();
+        if inherited.as_deref() != Some(prepared.initial_fresh_replay_key.as_slice()) {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+        }
+        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO game_durability_admission_lifecycle_receipts (operation_key,operation_json,decided_at) VALUES ($1,$2,$3)")
+            .bind(lifecycle_key(evidence)).bind(&prepared.encoded).bind(decided_at)
+            .execute(&mut **tx).await?;
+        // Reload through the existing canonical fresh receipt + current-row
+        // reconciler. The result is actual persisted state, never a snapshot
+        // manufactured from the requested successor or copied fixture data.
+        let current = self.current_session_locked(tx, *destination_game_session_id).await?;
+        if current.current_game_session_id() != *destination_game_session_id
+            || current.current_runtime_scope() != *destination_scope
+            || current.current_scope_generation().get() != *destination_scope_ownership_generation
+            || current.current_character_lease().generation()
+                != current_session.current_character_lease().generation().checked_add(1)
+                    .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?
+            || current.session_state() != GameSessionState::Reconnectable
+            || current.current_transport().is_some()
+            || current.current_connection_generation().get() != 1
+        {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+        }
+        Ok(current)
+    }
+}
+
+
+
+struct HouseSessionUseMembership {
+    character: CharacterId,
+    session: GameSessionId,
+    revision: u64,
+    operation_binding: [u8; 16],
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
+enum HouseSessionUseCheck {
+    OwningSuccessor { predecessor_revision: u64 },
+    HistoricalReceipt,
+}
+
+// Data validation shared by the actual post-write and historical read paths.
+// Its success is not a session-use grant: those paths still load the complete
+// native ledger and membership inside their protected transaction.
+fn validate_house_session_use_membership(
+    ledger: &SessionUseState,
+    membership: Option<&HouseSessionUseMembership>,
+    character: CharacterId,
+    session: GameSessionId,
+    operation_binding: [u8; 16],
+    check: HouseSessionUseCheck,
+) -> Result<()> {
+    let Some(membership) = membership else {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    };
+    if !ledger.complete
+        || membership.character != character
+        || membership.session != session
+        || operation_binding == [0; 16]
+        || membership.operation_binding != operation_binding
+        || membership.revision == 0
+        || ledger.revision > GAME_SESSION_USE_LEDGER_CAPACITY_V1
+        || membership.revision > ledger.revision
+    {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    if let HouseSessionUseCheck::OwningSuccessor { predecessor_revision } = check
+        && (predecessor_revision.checked_add(1) != Some(ledger.revision)
+            || membership.revision != ledger.revision)
+    {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+async fn verify_house_session_use_in_transaction(
+    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+    character: CharacterId,
+    session: GameSessionId,
+    operation_binding: [u8; 16],
+    check: HouseSessionUseCheck,
+) -> Result<()> {
+    use sqlx::Row;
+    // session_use_state checks the actual version, completeness, both high-water
+    // mirrors, complete membership count and maximum revision under the row lock.
+    let ledger = session_use_state(tx, character).await?;
+    let row = sqlx::query(
+        "SELECT uuid_send(character_id) AS character, uuid_send(game_session_id) AS session, \
+         membership_revision::text AS revision, \
+         CASE WHEN octet_length(operation_binding) = 16 THEN operation_binding END AS binding \
+         FROM game_durability_session_use_memberships WHERE game_session_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+    )
+    .bind(session.as_bytes().as_slice()).fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+    let membership = row.map(|row| -> Result<HouseSessionUseMembership> {
+        let binding: Option<Vec<u8>> = row.try_get("binding")?;
+        Ok(HouseSessionUseMembership {
+            character: checked(CharacterId::decode(&row.try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("character")?))?,
+            session: checked(GameSessionId::decode(&row.try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("session")?))?,
+            revision: checked(row.try_get::<String, _>("revision")?.parse::<u64>())?,
+            operation_binding: checked(binding.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?.try_into())?,
+        })
+    }).transpose()?;
+    validate_house_session_use_membership(
+        &ledger, membership.as_ref(), character, session, operation_binding, check,
+    )
+}
+
+/// A decoded committed result for retry/crash reconciliation. Its private
+/// history can be inspected, but it cannot prepare claims, place actors, bind a
+/// controller or establish the current scope owner after a restart.
+pub(super) struct HouseScopeClaimReceipt {
+    record: super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffRecord,
+    evidence: AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1,
+    decided_at: i64,
+}
+impl HouseScopeClaimReceipt {
+    pub(super) fn record(&self) -> &super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffRecord {
+        &self.record
+    }
+    pub(super) fn evidence(&self) -> &AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1 {
+        &self.evidence
+    }
+    pub(super) const fn decided_at(&self) -> i64 {
+        self.decided_at
+    }
+}
+
+impl FreshAdmissionStore {
+    /// Reconcile only existing durable history, without re-preparing or applying
+    /// a scope transition and without substituting it for current native ownership.
+    pub(super) async fn house_scope_transition_replay(
+        &self,
+        tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
+        commit: &super::house_scope_handoff::HouseEntryCommit,
+    ) -> std::result::Result<Option<HouseScopeClaimReceipt>, super::house_scope_handoff::HouseHandoffError> {
+        use sqlx::Row;
+        let Some(record) = super::house_scope_handoff::house_claim_replay_record_in_transaction(tx, commit).await? else {
+            return Ok(None);
+        };
+        // Only the key is derived from the independently retained native handoff.
+        // Expected guard rows and request DTOs never supply the stored operation.
+        let mut operation_key = b"claim-lifecycle-v1".to_vec();
+        operation_key.extend_from_slice(record.source_game_session_id.as_bytes());
+        operation_key.push(3); // D1362 provisional tag, re-cite after #2187.
+        operation_key.extend_from_slice(&record.handoff_id);
+        operation_key.extend_from_slice(commit.destination_game_session_id.as_bytes());
+        let stored = sqlx::query(
+            "SELECT CASE WHEN octet_length(operation_json) <= $2 THEN operation_json END AS payload, \
+             decided_at FROM game_durability_admission_lifecycle_receipts WHERE operation_key = $1",
+        )
+        .bind(&operation_key).bind(checked(i64::try_from(super::MAX_FRESH_OPERATION_BYTES))?)
+        .fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?;
+        let Some(stored) = stored else {
+            // Legacy handoffs have no D1362 lifecycle history. Their existence
+            // cannot be upgraded into a completed claim transition during replay.
+            return Ok(None);
+        };
+        let encoded: Option<String> = stored.try_get("payload")?;
+        let encoded = encoded.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+        let decided_at: i64 = stored.try_get("decided_at")?;
+        let initial_key: Option<Vec<u8>> = sqlx::query_scalar(
+            "SELECT COALESCE(fresh_replay_key,initial_fresh_replay_key) \
+             FROM game_durability_reconnect_sessions WHERE game_session_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+        )
+        .bind(record.source_game_session_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+        let initial_key = initial_key.ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+        let initial_receipt = self.receipt_locked(tx, &initial_key).await?
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+        let initial = checked(initial_receipt.binding().initial_commit())?;
+        let evidence = decode_house_lifecycle(&encoded, initial)?;
+        if evidence.operation.account_id() != initial_receipt.binding().account_id
+            || lifecycle_key(&evidence) != operation_key
+        {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+        }
+        checked(evidence.validate_historical(decided_at))?;
+        super::house_scope_handoff::validate_house_claim_history_in_transaction(
+            tx, &record, &evidence, decided_at,
+        ).await?;
+        verify_house_session_use_in_transaction(
+            tx, record.character_id, commit.destination_game_session_id, record.handoff_id,
+            HouseSessionUseCheck::HistoricalReceipt,
+        ).await?;
+        // Both predecessor and successor historical payloads must still exist
+        // exactly, even if their current projections have since advanced. Such
+        // newer projections are not rolled back or reused as replay permission.
+        for change in evidence.transition.predecessors.iter().chain(&evidence.transition.successors) {
+            let encoded_change = encode_guard(change, self.guards.maximum_guard_bytes)?;
+            let mut key = Writer::new(self.guards.maximum_guard_bytes);
+            write_key(&mut key, &change.key)?;
+            let exact: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(
+                "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM game_durability_admission_guard_history \
+                 WHERE guard_key = $1 AND publication_revision = $2::text::numeric(20,0) \
+                   AND source_authority = $3 AND source_revision = $4::text::numeric(20,0) \
+                   AND decision_identity = $5 AND change_json = $6)",
+            )
+            .bind(&key.bytes).bind(change.publication_revision.to_string())
+            .bind(&change.source.authority).bind(change.source.source_revision.to_string())
+            .bind(&change.source.decision_identity).bind(&encoded_change)
+            .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+            if !exact {
+                return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(Some(HouseScopeClaimReceipt { record, evidence, decided_at }))
     }
 }

+#[cfg(test)]
+mod house_scope_transition_codec_tests {
+    use super::*;
+
+    // Literal historical fixtures only. No sealed owner implements any live
+    // actor/placement permission in this codec test module.
+    fn history() -> Result<AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1> {
+        let id = |last| {
+            let mut bytes = [0; 16];
+            bytes[6] = 0x70;
+            bytes[8] = 0x80;
+            bytes[15] = last;
+            bytes
+        };
+        let account_id = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001".to_owned();
+        let character = checked(CharacterId::decode(&id(1)))?;
+        let world = checked(WorldId::decode(&id(2)))?;
+        let channel = checked(ChannelId::decode(&id(3)))?;
+        let source = checked(GameSessionId::decode(&id(4)))?;
+        let destination = checked(GameSessionId::decode(&id(5)))?;
+        let transport = checked(AuthenticatedTransportRefV1::decode(&[7; 16]))?;
+        let initial = checked(FreshAdmissionCommit::from_facts(
+            source,
+            checked(FreshAdmissionFacts::new([7; 32], character, world, channel, 2, 1))?,
+            transport,
+        ))?;
+        let current_session = checked(GameSessionAuthoritySnapshot::from_current_facts(
+            initial, GameSessionState::Active, checked(ConnectionGeneration::new(1))?,
+            Some(transport), checked(CharacterLease::new(character, 2))?,
+            Some(CharacterWorldEligibilityClaimV1::new(character, world)),
+            RuntimeScopeRefV1::channel(world, channel), checked(ScopeOwnershipGeneration::new(1))?,
+        ))?;
+        let house = super::super::house_scope_handoff::HouseId::new(
+            world, "oteryn:content.house.codec_fixture",
+        ).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+        let row_source = |purpose| AdmissionPublicationSourceV1 {
+            authority: "component-history-game-owner".into(), purpose,
+            source_revision: 8, decision_identity: "existing-admission-eight".into(),
+            source_observed_at: 100, clock_uncertainty_seconds: 0,
+        };
+        let predecessors = vec![
+            AdmissionAuthorityPublicationChangeV1 {
+                key: AdmissionAuthorityGuardKeyV1::Account { account_id: account_id.clone() },
+                source: row_source(AdmissionPublicationPurposeV1::AccountSecurityAndPresence),
+                precondition: AdmissionPublicationPreconditionV1::CompareAndSet { expected_publication_revision: 1 },
+                publication_revision: 2,
+                state: AdmissionAuthorityGuardStateV1::Account {
+                    security: FreshAccountSecurityObservationV1 {
+                        account_id: account_id.clone(), minimum_generation: 1, allowed: true,
+                        provenance: FreshEvidenceProvenanceV1 {
+                            source_authority: "component-history-platform".into(),
+                            purpose: FreshEvidencePurposeV1::PlatformSecurity,
+                            scope: Fnd04EvidenceScope::FreshAdmission,
+                            source_revision: 7, accepted_source_revision: 7,
+                            decision_identity: "platform-seven".into(),
+                            accepted_decision_identity: "platform-seven".into(),
+                            source_observed_at: 100, clock_uncertainty_seconds: 0,
+                            publication_revision: 2,
+                        },
+                    },
+                    presence: Some((character, source)),
+                },
+            },
+            AdmissionAuthorityPublicationChangeV1 {
+                key: AdmissionAuthorityGuardKeyV1::Character(character),
+                source: row_source(AdmissionPublicationPurposeV1::CharacterOwnershipAndLease),
+                precondition: AdmissionPublicationPreconditionV1::CompareAndSet { expected_publication_revision: 1 },
+                publication_revision: 2,
+                state: AdmissionAuthorityGuardStateV1::Character {
+                    account_id: account_id.clone(), world_id: world, eligible: true,
+                    lease_generation: 2, holder: Some(source),
+                },
+            },
+        ];
+        let mut successors = predecessors.clone();
+        for row in &mut successors {
+            row.precondition = AdmissionPublicationPreconditionV1::CompareAndSet { expected_publication_revision: 2 };
+            row.publication_revision = 3;
+            row.source.source_revision = 9;
+            row.source.decision_identity = "house-entry-nine".into();
+            match &mut row.state {
+                AdmissionAuthorityGuardStateV1::Account { security, presence } => {
+                    security.provenance.publication_revision = 3;
+                    *presence = Some((character, destination));
+                }
+                AdmissionAuthorityGuardStateV1::Character { lease_generation, holder, .. } => {
+                    *lease_generation = 3;
+                    *holder = Some(destination);
+                }
+                _ => return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState),
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1 {
+            operation: AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+                account_id, current_session, handoff_id: id(6),
+                destination_game_session_id: destination, destination_scope: house.runtime_scope()?,
+                destination_scope_ownership_generation: 1,
+                destination_house_key: house.house_key().into(), reserved_entry_tile: vec![2, 3, 4],
+            },
+            transition: AdmissionClaimTransitionEvidenceV1 { predecessors, successors, prepared_at: 100 },
+        })
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn house_scope_transition_codec_retains_exact_history_and_rejects_corruption() -> Result<()> {
+        let evidence = history()?;
+        let initial = evidence.operation.current_session().commit();
+        let encoded = encode_lifecycle(&evidence)?;
+        assert_eq!(decode_house_lifecycle(&encoded, initial)?, evidence);
+        let bytes = decode_envelope(&encoded, super::super::MAX_FRESH_OPERATION_BYTES)?;
+        for mutation in 0..3 {
+            let mut changed = bytes.clone();
+            match mutation {
+                0 => changed[0] = 255,
+                1 => { changed.pop(); }
+                _ => changed.push(0),
+            }
+            let changed = encode_envelope(&changed, super::super::MAX_FRESH_OPERATION_BYTES)?;
+            assert!(decode_house_lifecycle(&changed, initial).is_err(), "wire mutation {mutation}");
+        }
+        // A different independently supplied immutable initial receipt must not
+        // be replaced by the historical initial fields from the payload.
+        let wrong_initial = checked(FreshAdmissionCommit::from_facts(
+            initial.game_session_id(),
+            checked(FreshAdmissionFacts::new([8; 32], initial.character_id(), initial.world_id(),
+                initial.channel_id(), initial.character_lease_generation() + 1,
+                initial.scope_ownership_generation()))?,
+            initial.initial_transport(),
+        ))?;
+        assert!(decode_house_lifecycle(&encoded, wrong_initial).is_err());
+        Ok(())
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn house_scope_transition_codec_binds_native_house_identity_and_finite_tile() -> Result<()> {
+        let evidence = history()?;
+        let initial = evidence.operation.current_session().commit();
+        for mutation in 0..3 {
+            let mut changed = evidence.clone();
+            let AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+                destination_house_key, reserved_entry_tile, ..
+            } = &mut changed.operation else {
+                return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+            };
+            match mutation {
+                0 => destination_house_key.push_str("_other"),
+                1 => *reserved_entry_tile = vec![1; super::super::house_scope_handoff::HOUSE_TILE_MAX_BYTES + 1],
+                _ => *reserved_entry_tile = vec![1; super::super::MAX_FRESH_OPERATION_BYTES],
+            }
+            match encode_lifecycle(&changed) {
+                Ok(encoded) => assert!(decode_house_lifecycle(&encoded, initial).is_err(),
+                    "native identity/length mutation {mutation}"),
+                Err(_) => assert_eq!(mutation, 2, "unexpected failure before decode"),
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn house_scope_transition_membership_requires_one_bound_native_advance() -> Result<()> {
+        let evidence = history()?;
+        let AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+            current_session, destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, ..
+        } = evidence.operation else {
+            return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState);
+        };
+        let character = current_session.commit().character_id();
+        let membership = HouseSessionUseMembership {
+            character, session: destination_game_session_id, revision: 8,
+            operation_binding: handoff_id,
+        };
+        let ledger = SessionUseState { revision: 8, complete: true };
+        let owning = HouseSessionUseCheck::OwningSuccessor { predecessor_revision: 7 };
+        validate_house_session_use_membership(&ledger, Some(&membership), character,
+            destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, owning)?;
+        for mutation in 0..8 {
+            let mut changed_ledger = SessionUseState { revision: ledger.revision, complete: ledger.complete };
+            let mut changed = HouseSessionUseMembership {
+                character: membership.character, session: membership.session,
+                revision: membership.revision, operation_binding: membership.operation_binding,
+            };
+            let mut check = owning;
+            match mutation {
+                0 => changed_ledger.complete = false,
+                1 => changed_ledger.revision = 9,
+                2 => changed.revision = 7,
+                3 => changed.revision = 0,
+                4 => changed.character = checked(CharacterId::decode(current_session.current_game_session_id().as_bytes()))?,
+                5 => changed.session = current_session.current_game_session_id(),
+                6 => changed.operation_binding[15] ^= 1,
+                _ => check = HouseSessionUseCheck::OwningSuccessor { predecessor_revision: u64::MAX },
+            }
+            assert!(validate_house_session_use_membership(&changed_ledger, Some(&changed),
+                character, destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, check).is_err(),
+                "changed native session-use effect {mutation}");
+        }
+        assert!(validate_house_session_use_membership(&ledger, None, character,
+            destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, owning).is_err());
+        // A later complete ledger may retain this exact committed history. That
+        // is accepted only by the history path; it cannot satisfy owning CAS.
+        let later = SessionUseState { revision: 9, complete: true };
+        validate_house_session_use_membership(&later, Some(&membership), character,
+            destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, HouseSessionUseCheck::HistoricalReceipt)?;
+        assert!(validate_house_session_use_membership(&later, Some(&membership), character,
+            destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, owning).is_err());
+        let regressed = SessionUseState { revision: 7, complete: true };
+        assert!(validate_house_session_use_membership(&regressed, Some(&membership), character,
+            destination_game_session_id, handoff_id, HouseSessionUseCheck::HistoricalReceipt).is_err());
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}

+
+/// D1362 preparation facts read from the locked native handoff and its owners.
+/// This is not a placement capability. Its interior is one statement snapshot;
+/// only the future native House actor owner may consume it for materialization.
+pub(super) struct PreparedHouseClaimContext {
+    record: HouseHandoffRecord,
+    account_id: String,
+    origin_channel: crate::foundation::ChannelId,
+    source_connection_generation: u64,
+    source_character_lease_generation: u64,
+    source_scope_ownership_generation: u64,
+    destination_scope_ownership_generation: u64,
+    destination_game_session_id: GameSessionId,
+    node: NodeIncarnationProof,
+    interior: super::house_interior::HouseInteriorSnapshot,
+}
+
+impl PreparedHouseClaimContext {
+    pub(super) fn source_session(&self) -> GameSessionId {
+        self.record.source_game_session_id
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn interior(&self) -> &super::house_interior::HouseInteriorSnapshot {
+        &self.interior
+    }
+
+    /// The adapter supplies the independently reconciled current session from
+    /// this same transaction. Expected request fields never construct it.
+    pub(super) fn operation(
+        &self,
+        current: crate::foundation::GameSessionAuthoritySnapshot<
+            crate::foundation::AuthenticatedTransportRefV1,
+        >,
+    ) -> Result<crate::foundation::admission_authority_publication::AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1> {
+        use crate::foundation::GameSessionState;
+        if current.current_game_session_id() != self.record.source_game_session_id
+            || current.commit().character_id() != self.record.character_id
+            || current.commit().world_id() != self.record.house.world_id()
+            || current.current_connection_generation().get() != self.source_connection_generation
+            || current.current_character_lease().generation() != self.source_character_lease_generation
+            || current.current_scope_generation().get() != self.source_scope_ownership_generation
+            || current.current_runtime_scope()
+                != RuntimeScopeRefV1::channel(self.record.house.world_id(), self.origin_channel)
+            || !matches!(current.session_state(), GameSessionState::Active | GameSessionState::Reconnectable)
+        {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+        }
+        Ok(crate::foundation::admission_authority_publication::AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+            account_id: self.account_id.clone(),
+            current_session: current,
+            handoff_id: self.record.handoff_id,
+            destination_game_session_id: self.destination_game_session_id,
+            destination_scope: self.record.house.runtime_scope()?,
+            destination_scope_ownership_generation: self.destination_scope_ownership_generation,
+            destination_house_key: self.record.house.house_key().into(),
+            reserved_entry_tile: self.record.reserved_tile.clone(),
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// Verify only the durable successor written by the owning handoff. This
+    /// deliberately supplies no placement proof from terminal/generation state.
+    /// The D1362 caller must have completed actual placement and reservation
+    /// consumption before reaching its bind/generation/claim write sequence.
+    pub(super) async fn verify_durable_successor(
+        &self,
+        tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,
+    ) -> Result<()> {
+        let (current, _) = load_handoff(tx, self.record.handoff_id, true)
+            .await?
+            .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+        let mut expected = self.record.clone();
+        // The reservation must already be consumed while the destination is
+        // still unbound at generation one. The legacy release path cannot
+        // establish this state: terminal or generation > 1 is never accepted
+        // here as a substitute for the missing native placement producer.
+        expected.state = HouseHandoffState::Released;
+        expected.destination_game_session_id = Some(self.destination_game_session_id);
+        if current != expected
+            || !house_assigned(tx, &self.record.house, self.destination_scope_ownership_generation, Some(&self.node)).await?
+        {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+        }
+        let correct: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(
+            "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM game_durability_reconnect_sessions s \
+             JOIN game_durability_reconnect_sessions d \
+               ON d.game_session_id = encode($2,'hex')::uuid \
+             WHERE s.game_session_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid \
+               AND s.account_id = $3::uuid AND d.account_id = s.account_id \
+               AND s.character_id = encode($4,'hex')::uuid AND d.character_id = s.character_id \
+               AND s.world_id = encode($5,'hex')::uuid AND d.world_id = s.world_id \
+               AND s.session_state = 3 AND s.current_transport_ref IS NULL \
+               AND s.current_generation = $6::text::numeric(20,0) \
+               AND s.character_lease_generation = $7::text::numeric(20,0) \
+               AND s.scope_ownership_generation = $8::text::numeric(20,0) \
+               AND s.runtime_scope_kind = 1 AND s.runtime_scope_world_id = s.world_id \
+               AND s.runtime_scope_channel_id = encode($9,'hex')::uuid \
+               AND d.session_state = 1 AND d.current_transport_ref IS NULL \
+               AND d.current_generation = 1 \
+               AND d.character_lease_generation = $10::text::numeric(20,0) \
+               AND d.scope_ownership_generation = $11::text::numeric(20,0) \
+               AND d.runtime_scope_kind = 2 AND d.runtime_scope_world_id = d.world_id \
+               AND d.runtime_scope_house_key = $12 \
+               AND d.runtime_scope_instance_id = encode($13,'hex')::uuid \
+               AND d.origin_channel_id = s.runtime_scope_channel_id)",
+        )
+        .bind(self.record.source_game_session_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(self.destination_game_session_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(&self.account_id)
+        .bind(self.record.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(self.record.house.world_id().as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(self.source_connection_generation.to_string())
+        .bind(self.source_character_lease_generation.to_string())
+        .bind(self.source_scope_ownership_generation.to_string())
+        .bind(self.origin_channel.as_bytes().as_slice())
+        .bind(self.source_character_lease_generation.checked_add(1).ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?.to_string())
+        .bind(self.destination_scope_ownership_generation.to_string())
+        .bind(self.record.house.house_key())
+        .bind(self.record.house.instance_id().as_slice())
+        .fetch_one(&mut **tx)
+        .await?;
+        if !correct {
+            return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}
+
+/// D1362 native preparation, before any source terminalization or claim effect.
+/// The caller already owns the recovery fence and admission relation locks.
+/// No controller or placement acknowledgement is constructed from these facts.
+pub(super) async fn prepare_house_claim_context_in_transaction(
+    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,
+    node: &NodeIncarnationProof,
+    commit: &HouseEntryCommit,
+) -> Result<PreparedHouseClaimContext> {
+    if !is_uuid_v7(&commit.handoff_id) || commit.committed_at < 0 {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::InvalidInput);
+    }
+    let row = sqlx::query(
+        "SELECT state, account_id::text AS account, uuid_send(origin_channel_id) AS origin, \
+         source_connection_generation::text AS source_generation, \
+         source_character_lease_generation::text AS source_lease, \
+         source_scope_ownership_generation::text AS source_scope, \
+         destination_scope_ownership_generation::text AS house_generation, prepared_at \
+         FROM game_house_scope_handoffs WHERE handoff_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid FOR UPDATE",
+    )
+    .bind(commit.handoff_id.as_slice())
+    .fetch_optional(&mut **tx)
+    .await?
+    .ok_or(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected)?;
+    let (record, _) = load_handoff(tx, commit.handoff_id, false)
+        .await?
+        .ok_or(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    if row.try_get::<i16, _>("state")? != HANDOFF_PREPARED
+        || record.state != HouseHandoffState::Prepared
+        || record.destination_game_session_id.is_some()
+        || record.source_game_session_id == commit.destination_game_session_id
+        || row.try_get::<i64, _>("prepared_at")? > commit.committed_at
+    {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+    }
+    let source_scope = parse_u64(&row.try_get::<String, _>("source_scope")?)?;
+    let house_generation = parse_u64(&row.try_get::<String, _>("house_generation")?)?;
+    let origin: Vec<u8> = row.try_get("origin")?;
+    let origin_channel = crate::foundation::ChannelId::decode(&origin)
+        .map_err(|_| DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState)?;
+    let mut origin_key = vec![1_u8];
+    origin_key.extend_from_slice(record.house.world_id().as_bytes());
+    origin_key.extend_from_slice(origin_channel.as_bytes());
+    if !house_assigned(tx, &record.house, house_generation, Some(node)).await?
+        || !origin_channel_held(tx, &origin_key, source_scope, node).await?
+    {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+    }
+    let source_locked: Option<i32> = sqlx::query_scalar(
+        "SELECT 1 FROM game_durability_reconnect_sessions \
+         WHERE game_session_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid \
+           AND character_id = encode($2,'hex')::uuid \
+           AND world_id = encode($3,'hex')::uuid \
+           AND account_id = $4::uuid AND session_state IN (1,2) \
+           AND runtime_scope_kind = 1 AND runtime_scope_world_id = world_id \
+           AND runtime_scope_channel_id = encode($5,'hex')::uuid \
+           AND current_generation = $6::text::numeric(20,0) \
+           AND character_lease_generation = $7::text::numeric(20,0) \
+           AND scope_ownership_generation = $8::text::numeric(20,0) FOR UPDATE",
+    )
+    .bind(record.source_game_session_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+    .bind(record.character_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+    .bind(record.house.world_id().as_bytes().as_slice())
+    .bind(row.try_get::<String, _>("account")?)
+    .bind(origin_channel.as_bytes().as_slice())
+    .bind(row.try_get::<String, _>("source_generation")?)
+    .bind(row.try_get::<String, _>("source_lease")?)
+    .bind(source_scope.to_string())
+    .fetch_optional(&mut **tx)
+    .await?;
+    if source_locked != Some(1) {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+    }
+    let access = sqlx::query(
+        "SELECT a.role, a.content_fenced, \
+         a.acl_revision IS NOT DISTINCT FROM h.acl_revision AS acl_current, \
+         a.guild_revisions IS NOT DISTINCT FROM h.guild_revisions AS guild_current \
+         FROM game_house_scope_handoffs h \
+         CROSS JOIN LATERAL game_house_access(h.world_id,h.house_key,h.character_id) a \
+         WHERE h.handoff_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid",
+    )
+    .bind(commit.handoff_id.as_slice())
+    .fetch_all(&mut **tx)
+    .await?;
+    let [access] = access.as_slice() else {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+    };
+    if !access.try_get::<Option<String>, _>("role")?.as_deref().is_some_and(|role| HOUSE_ACCESS_ROLES.contains(&role))
+        || access.try_get::<Option<bool>, _>("content_fenced")? != Some(false)
+        || !access.try_get::<bool, _>("acl_current")?
+        || !access.try_get::<bool, _>("guild_current")?
+    {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::AuthorityRejected);
+    }
+    let interior = super::house_interior::load_house_interior_in_transaction(
+        tx, &record.house, house_generation, node,
+    )
+    .await
+    .map_err(HouseHandoffError::InteriorUnavailable)?;
+    Ok(PreparedHouseClaimContext {
+        record,
+        account_id: row.try_get("account")?,
+        origin_channel,
+        source_connection_generation: parse_u64(&row.try_get::<String, _>("source_generation")?)?,
+        source_character_lease_generation: parse_u64(&row.try_get::<String, _>("source_lease")?)?,
+        source_scope_ownership_generation: source_scope,
+        destination_scope_ownership_generation: house_generation,
+        destination_game_session_id: commit.destination_game_session_id,
+        node: node.clone(),
+        interior,
+    })
+}
+
+
+/// Independently retained native identity for historical reconciliation only.
+/// Replaying a committed record cannot establish current placement or ownership.
+pub(super) async fn house_claim_replay_record_in_transaction(
+    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,
+    commit: &HouseEntryCommit,
+) -> Result<Option<HouseHandoffRecord>> {
+    if !is_uuid_v7(&commit.handoff_id) || commit.committed_at < 0 {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::InvalidInput);
+    }
+    let Some((record, _)) = load_handoff(tx, commit.handoff_id, true).await? else {
+        return Ok(None);
+    };
+    if record.state == HouseHandoffState::Prepared {
+        return Ok(None);
+    }
+    if record.destination_game_session_id != Some(commit.destination_game_session_id) {
+        return Err(HouseHandoffError::InvalidInput);
+    }
+    Ok(Some(record))
+}
+
+/// Compare decoded history with independently retained native handoff fields.
+/// This method never returns a live admission or a materialization capability.
+pub(super) async fn validate_house_claim_history_in_transaction(
+    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,
+    record: &HouseHandoffRecord,
+    evidence: &crate::foundation::admission_authority_publication::AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1,
+    decided_at: i64,
+) -> Result<()> {
+    use crate::foundation::admission_authority_publication::AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1;
+    let AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition {
+        account_id, current_session, handoff_id, destination_game_session_id,
+        destination_scope, destination_scope_ownership_generation,
+        destination_house_key, reserved_entry_tile,
+    } = &evidence.operation else {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+    };
+    if *handoff_id != record.handoff_id
+        || record.source_game_session_id != current_session.current_game_session_id()
+        || record.character_id != current_session.commit().character_id()
+        || record.destination_game_session_id != Some(*destination_game_session_id)
+        || record.house.house_key() != destination_house_key
+        || record.house.runtime_scope()? != *destination_scope
+        || record.reserved_tile != *reserved_entry_tile
+        || record.state != HouseHandoffState::Released
+    {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+    }
+    let RuntimeScopeRefV1::Channel { world_id, channel_id } = current_session.current_runtime_scope() else {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+    };
+    let exact: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(
+        "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM game_house_scope_handoffs \
+         WHERE handoff_id = encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND state = 3 \
+           AND account_id = $2::uuid AND world_id = encode($3,'hex')::uuid \
+           AND origin_channel_id = encode($4,'hex')::uuid \
+           AND source_connection_generation = $5::text::numeric(20,0) \
+           AND source_character_lease_generation = $6::text::numeric(20,0) \
+           AND source_scope_ownership_generation = $7::text::numeric(20,0) \
+           AND destination_scope_ownership_generation = $8::text::numeric(20,0) \
+           AND prepared_at <= $9 AND committed_at <= $10)",
+    )
+    .bind(handoff_id.as_slice()).bind(account_id)
+    .bind(world_id.as_bytes().as_slice()).bind(channel_id.as_bytes().as_slice())
+    .bind(current_session.current_connection_generation().get().to_string())
+    .bind(current_session.current_character_lease().generation().to_string())
+    .bind(current_session.current_scope_generation().get().to_string())
+    .bind(destination_scope_ownership_generation.to_string())
+    .bind(evidence.transition.prepared_at).bind(decided_at)
+    .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+    if !exact {
+        return Err(DurabilityError::InvalidStoredState.into());
+    }
+    Ok(())
 }

+/// Inert claim successors for an owning native House handoff. Only a registered
+/// source can prepare them; the adapter must still independently load the current
+/// handoff, session and canonical guard history inside the one physical transaction.
+/// Actor placement and reservation consumption must precede controller bind.
+/// ```compile_fail
+/// use oteryn_game_server::foundation::admission_authority_publication::*;
+/// fn forge(history: AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1) -> HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1 {
+///     HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1::from(history)
+/// }
+/// ```
+#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
+pub struct HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1 {
+    evidence: AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1,
+}
+
+impl HouseScopeTransitionClaimV1 {
+    pub fn prepare(
+        owner: &dyn AdmissionClaimOwningSourceV1,
+        operation: AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1,
+        now: i64,
+    ) -> Result<Self, AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1> {
+        if !matches!(operation, AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1::HouseScopeTransition { .. }) {
+            return Err(AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1::Invalid);
+        }
+        Ok(Self { evidence: prepare_lifecycle(owner, operation, now)? })
+    }
+
+    #[must_use]
+    pub const fn evidence(&self) -> &AdmissionClaimLifecycleEvidenceV1 {
+        &self.evidence
+    }
+
+    /// `current_operation` comes from the independently locked native handoff and
+    /// current session, never from `self.evidence()`. The guard loader must have
+    /// checked each complete projection against its immutable publication history.
+    pub fn validate_locked(
+        &self,
+        current_operation: &AdmissionClaimLifecycleOperationV1,
+        rows: &[Option<AdmissionAuthorityPublicationChangeV1>],
+        now: i64,
+    ) -> Result<&[AdmissionAuthorityPublicationChangeV1], AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1> {
+        if current_operation != &self.evidence.operation {
+            return Err(AdmissionAuthorityPublicationErrorV1::Stale);
+        }
+        validate_lifecycle_locked(&self.evidence, rows, current_operation.current_session(), now)?;
+        Ok(&self.evidence.transition.successors)
+    }
+}
+
