````diff
+use crate::content::{LogicalCell, QualifiedNativeEntryRoom, encode_spatial_position};
+use crate::durability::fresh_admission::PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims;
+use crate::durability::house_interior::{HOUSE_INTERIOR_ITEMS_MAX, HouseInteriorSnapshot};
+use crate::foundation::{
+    ChannelRuntimeV1, ExactActorRef, GameSessionState, HouseActorAuthorityV1,
+    HouseActorDirectory, HouseEntryPlacement, HousePlacementError, HouseSourceActorHold,
+    MovementLocalPosition, RuntimeScopeRefV1,
+};
+use sqlx::{Postgres, Row, Transaction};
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+#[derive(Debug)]
+pub(crate) enum HouseEntryStageError {
+    NativeAuthorityMismatch,
+    QualifiedHouseUnavailable,
+    ContentBindingMismatch,
+    InteriorLocationMismatch,
+    Placement(HousePlacementError),
+    Database(sqlx::Error),
+}
+impl From<sqlx::Error> for HouseEntryStageError {
+    fn from(error: sqlx::Error) -> Self { Self::Database(error) }
+}
+impl From<HousePlacementError> for HouseEntryStageError {
+    fn from(error: HousePlacementError) -> Self { Self::Placement(error) }
+}
+
+/// Exact qualified source identity retained alongside the original loader
+/// snapshot. Neither this value nor 0047 history grants active Content or ADMIT.
+struct StagedHouseContentIdentity {
+    house_revision: String,
+    server_artifact_digest: [u8; 32],
+    qualified_placement_digest: [u8; 32],
+}
+
+/// Actual native materialization preparation with both physical owners held.
+/// There is deliberately no bind/commit method at this boundary: the current
+/// ChannelSpellStates owner has not transferred its actual PlayerSpellState,
+/// and the ordered FND/ingress/active-Content integration is still held by CP.
+/// Dropping the stage releases the same House slot and exact source fence.
+/// Borrowing the real SQL transaction prevents its caller from committing it
+/// behind this preparation's back while the physical reservations remain live.
+pub(crate) struct StagedHouseEntry<'owner, 'transaction, 'connection> {
+    transaction: &'transaction mut Transaction<'connection, Postgres>,
+    prepared: PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims,
+    placement: HouseEntryPlacement<'owner>,
+    source_hold: HouseSourceActorHold<'owner>,
+    content: StagedHouseContentIdentity,
+}
+
+impl StagedHouseEntry<'_, '_, '_> {
+    /// The actual #2163 loader result is retained by the owning stage. It is
+    /// never reconstructed from expected HouseItem DTOs or an empty fallback.
+    pub(crate) fn interior(&self) -> &HouseInteriorSnapshot { self.prepared.interior() }
+
+    pub(crate) fn reserved_tile(&self) -> &[u8] { self.prepared.reserved_tile() }
+
+    /// Revalidate the exact physical reservation and original native TX. This
+    /// writes no receipt, session, lease, transport binding or generation.
+    pub(crate) async fn verify_consumed_in_owning_transaction(&mut self) -> Result<(), HouseEntryStageError> {
+        verify_stage_transaction(self.transaction, &self.prepared).await?;
+        self.placement.verify_consumed(&self.prepared)?;
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}
+
+async fn verify_stage_transaction(
+    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,
+    prepared: &PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims,
+) -> Result<(), HouseEntryStageError> {
+    let actual: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT txid_current()::text")
+        .fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;
+    if actual != prepared.transaction_identity() {
+        return Err(HouseEntryStageError::NativeAuthorityMismatch);
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+fn validate_loaded_interior(
+    interior: &HouseInteriorSnapshot,
+    house: &HouseId,
+    generation: u64,
+    qualified_cells: &BTreeSet<Vec<u8>>,
+) -> Result<(), HouseEntryStageError> {
+    if &interior.house != house || interior.ownership_generation != generation
+        || u64::try_from(interior.items.len()).ok().is_none_or(|count| count > HOUSE_INTERIOR_ITEMS_MAX)
+        || interior.items.iter().any(|item| !qualified_cells.contains(&item.spatial_position))
+    {
+        return Err(HouseEntryStageError::InteriorLocationMismatch);
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+/// Consume the actual native preparation in its caller-owned SQL transaction.
+/// `destination` is only a proposal: its canonical bytes must equal the locked
+/// PREPARED tile, its real qualified House cells must match the immutable 0047
+/// binding, and the source actor/lease/pin are read from their current owner.
+/// The accepted loader must have succeeded before this function can be called.
+///
+/// This is a real physical stage, not current-content/ADMIT activation. The
+/// native full-bundle House catalogue is Unknown today and therefore refuses;
+/// an explicitly qualified source can be used for isolated qualification after
+/// #2167 and the narrow shared read accessors land. No entry-catalogue fallback
+/// is substituted for Unknown full-bundle metadata.
+pub(crate) async fn stage_house_entry_in_transaction<'owner, 'transaction, 'connection>(
+    tx: &'transaction mut Transaction<'connection, Postgres>,
+    prepared: PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims,
+    room: &QualifiedNativeEntryRoom,
+    source_runtime: &'owner mut ChannelRuntimeV1,
+    source_actor: ExactActorRef,
+    destination: MovementLocalPosition,
+    house_actors: &'owner mut HouseActorDirectory,
+) -> Result<StagedHouseEntry<'owner, 'transaction, 'connection>, HouseEntryStageError> {
+    verify_stage_transaction(tx, &prepared).await?;
+    let current = prepared.source_authority();
+    let binding = source_runtime.binding();
+    let pinned = source_runtime.content_pin();
+    if current.session_state() != GameSessionState::Active
+        || current.current_transport().is_none()
+        || current.current_control_loss_epoch().is_some()
+        || current.current_game_session_id() != prepared.source_session()
+        || current.current_character_lease() != prepared.source_character_lease()
+        || current.current_runtime_scope() != RuntimeScopeRefV1::channel(binding.world_id(), binding.channel_id())
+        || current.current_scope_generation() != binding.scope_generation()
+        || prepared.house().world_id() != binding.world_id()
+        || source_runtime.bound_attacker_lease(source_actor, prepared.source_session())
+            .map_err(|_| HouseEntryStageError::NativeAuthorityMismatch)? != prepared.source_character_lease()
+        || source_runtime.positioned_player_for_session(prepared.source_session())
+            .map_err(|_| HouseEntryStageError::NativeAuthorityMismatch)?
+            .is_none_or(|(actor, position)| actor != source_actor || position.context() != source_runtime.pinned_movement_context())
+    {
+        return Err(HouseEntryStageError::NativeAuthorityMismatch);
+    }
+    let cells = room.movement_cells();
+    if cells.scope().world_id != binding.world_id()
+        || cells.scope().generation_digest != pinned.server_artifact_digest()
+        || room.compiled().server_digest() != pinned.server_artifact_digest()
+        || room.compiled().client_digest() != pinned.client_artifact_digest()
+        || room.frame_binding().digest() != pinned.frame_binding_digest()
+        || room.map_revision_digest() != pinned.map_revision_digest()
+        || encode_spatial_position(destination) != prepared.reserved_tile()
+    {
+        return Err(HouseEntryStageError::ContentBindingMismatch);
+    }
+    let house = cells.house_tiles().house_at(cells.scope(), LogicalCell {
+        x: destination.x, y: destination.y, z: i32::from(destination.floor),
+    }).map_err(|_| HouseEntryStageError::QualifiedHouseUnavailable)?
+        .ok_or(HouseEntryStageError::QualifiedHouseUnavailable)?;
+    if house.house().key != prepared.house().house_key() {
+        return Err(HouseEntryStageError::ContentBindingMismatch);
+    }
+    // entry() is an exterior fallback in the accepted qualifier. It is not
+    // used as the interior reservation; house_at() proves actual membership.
+    let content = StagedHouseContentIdentity {
+        house_revision: house.house().revision.clone(),
+        server_artifact_digest: pinned.server_artifact_digest(),
+        qualified_placement_digest: house.placement_digest(),
+    };
+    let allocated = sqlx::query(
+        "SELECT h.house_revision,h.compatible_content_digest,h.qualified_placement_digest \
+         FROM game_world_house_instances h JOIN game_house_ownership o USING(world_id,house_key) \
+         WHERE h.world_id=encode($1,'hex')::uuid AND h.house_key=$2 \
+           AND h.instance_id=encode($3,'hex')::uuid FOR SHARE OF h,o",
+    )
+    .bind(prepared.house().world_id().as_bytes().as_slice())
+    .bind(prepared.house().house_key())
+    .bind(prepared.house().instance_id().as_slice())
+    .fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?
+    .ok_or(HouseEntryStageError::ContentBindingMismatch)?;
+    if allocated.try_get::<String, _>("house_revision")? != content.house_revision
+        || allocated.try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("compatible_content_digest")?.as_slice() != content.server_artifact_digest
+        || allocated.try_get::<Vec<u8>, _>("qualified_placement_digest")?.as_slice() != content.qualified_placement_digest
+        || content.server_artifact_digest == [0; 32] || content.qualified_placement_digest == [0; 32]
+    {
+        return Err(HouseEntryStageError::ContentBindingMismatch);
+    }
+    // Read only this actual qualified House (at most 4096 cells), once. Every
+    // native loaded location must be in it; no global catalogue reset/scan and
+    // no copied spatial encoder are used to validate the retained snapshot.
+    let qualified_cells = house.physical_cells().map(|cell| {
+        crate::content::checked_movement_position(cell)
+            .map(encode_spatial_position)
+            .ok_or(HouseEntryStageError::ContentBindingMismatch)
+    }).collect::<Result<BTreeSet<_>, _>>()?;
+    validate_loaded_interior(prepared.interior(), prepared.house(), prepared.scope_generation().get(), &qualified_cells)?;
+    let source_hold = source_runtime.hold_house_source_actor(source_actor, prepared.source_session())?;
+    let mut placement = house_actors.reserve_entry(&prepared)?;
+    placement.materialize(&source_hold)?;
+    placement.consume_reservation()?;
+    placement.verify_consumed(&prepared)?;
+    Ok(StagedHouseEntry { transaction: tx, prepared, placement, source_hold, content })
+}
+

+// Only this native, privately constructed PREPARED owner implements the

+    fn destination_session(&self) -> GameSessionId { self.context.destination_session() }

+    fn reserved_tile(&self) -> &[u8] { &self.context.record().reserved_tile }
````

### Artifact `actor2165-additional-owner-request.md`

Original bytes: 6815; SHA-256 `135b4d7b95f6b093b4fdc9a3e08c49d10f41e5b35f7ebac7ae1d28d1fbe9a22d`; encoding `utf8`.

<!-- BEGIN ARTIFACT actor2165-additional-owner-request.md utf8 135b4d7b95f6b093b4fdc9a3e08c49d10f41e5b35f7ebac7ae1d28d1fbe9a22d 6815 -->
````markdown
# #2165 D1373 — exact additional shared source seams and retained gates

Root CP request draft; no requested shared hunk has been published by this builder.
D1373 grants the actual House actor producer and consumer to #2165, with one existing
carrier, and fixes merge/activation order: #2197 → #2198 → #2187 → SESSION-INGRESS-1
(D1369) → House consumer. Current main observed by root is ce7bccab973625d5d3d0d0c566426903229d2373.
The owned staged source is complete as a reviewable proposal; production entry/login
remain HOUSE_CLOSED and exit remains ExitNotAdmitted.

## Allocation requested

1. `foundation/mod.rs`: only the exact named re-exports below; the carrier stays private.
   Standalone Foundation roots require the pure native House identity without importing
   the SQL-heavy Durability tree. Move the existing HouseId and its exact byte encoder
   into the one owned `house_scope/actor_placement.rs` mount; re-export the same type and
   three existing constants through Foundation and the old Durability public path.
   Keep the old Durability-facing runtime_scope() implementation there. No second
   HouseId, cloned encoder, Channel-as-House surrogate or new constructor semantics.
2. `foundation/npc_actor.rs`: one field access uses the already-validated continuity's
   ChannelId after the carrier's private field becomes Channel | House(HouseId).
   NPC/Channel behavior and all other shared consumers stay unchanged.
3. D1362's existing native prepare object: only the private native typed key/generation,
   read-only views and sealed actor-binding implementation below. Its constructor stays
   Durability-private and reads the actual locked PREPARED/guard/session/interior facts.
   The owned consumer holds the actual SQL transaction and both physical owner guards;
   it does not gain a commit/bind method or an active Content grant.
4. After #2167's actual source lands, `content/project/native_house_tiles.rs`: two
   read-only accessors on an already-qualified single House. placement_digest() delegates
   to its ONE existing canonical encoder; physical_cells() iterates only that House's
   finite set. The consumer uses #2167's canonical spatial encoder and exact 0047
   identity. No full catalogue scan, copied codec, numeric-key invention or Unknown
   full-bundle fallback. The exact #2167 source dependency is disclosed below.

Do these exact dependency hunks have the same held D1373 slot allocation? The already
granted minimal carrier delta is included separately for audit, not as an activation request.

## Additional real owner boundary requiring assignment

The source Slot contains real ActorState, VersionedPosition, lifecycle/conditions and
ActorCombatState. It does not own PlayerSpellState. Actual current `gameplay_transport/
actor_spell.rs` blob 6beedb78cb2ac95de4f6332b3e6d0537f93d2e75 stores its private
Vec<(ExactActorRef, GameSessionId, PlayerSpellState)> at line 94. All get/initialize
paths require a current Channel player; initialize prunes a removed source and would
create default vitals/cooldowns for an absent key. There is no take/transfer/adopt port.

Please assign the actual owner seam to #2165 or name its owner: source runtime lock
first, then the existing spell owner; exact current actor/session, idle/pending/death
checks; move the existing complete PlayerSpellState into a rollback token before the
source becomes unreachable; retain it across unknown COMMIT; restore the exact state
on abort; adopt it only through an actual House-aware current physical handle. This
must preserve existing cooldowns/vitals/conditions/regen and resolve pending item-use,
training, familiar/timer and owner-custody constraints through their real owners.
A new default PlayerSpellState or second actor carrier is not a solution. No speculative
spell-owner implementation is included in this request.

The current owned stage intentionally has NO bind/COMMIT API, so this unresolved
boundary cannot lose PlayerSpellState or promote a partial actor to gameplay. It has
actual native PREPARED/TX, qualified-cell/0047 checks, real Slot reservation/materialization/
consumption and retained original #2163 interior snapshot. Source rollback on drop stays
safe. It writes no session, receipt, lease, transport or generation.

Current native full-bundle House metadata remains Unknown. QualifiedHousePlacement::entry()
is explicitly outside House cells (the qualifier rejects placed.contains(entry)); the
consumer therefore validates the real PREPARED destination with house_at()/contains,
not that exterior fallback. Active Content/current full-bundle, future actual item
collision/materialization and process-restart Character reconstruction remain owning
integration gates. A durable receipt alone cannot reconstruct missing physical state.

## Source review / qualification

Independent SOURCE REVIEW PASS covers core3 actor SHA256
`de655311758bdc85a3bb67a529467d250add3039df8b328d9475b35cda8020b4`,
manifest `dea165753be3bd13ab5060dfaf37a4720c395056a4e3e5db8f148143197601df`.
The inventory now contains 15 pure cases, currently NOT_RUN. Review repairs preserve
the actual arena invariant at generation exhaustion, reject Released against another
source Channel, allow denial-only cleanup in the retained retired namespace, bind
node/revision again at consumption, and refuse to discard a source whose complete
Slot payload changed after an unknown outcome. These checks do not claim that the
existing attack fence freezes every future ingress or cadence owner.

The separately frozen actual native stage also received SOURCE REVIEW PASS at
manifest `b85bd235cfb60c3f2b7aa009ea234055eeda9139546e0ce677bd10ff4f37b9ba` and
patch `800caa75490a65aa6b5de75455a380f7b34a1bee5ca60f1408e8036cd5ed0926`.
The actual #2167 and SQL schema sources were read by the independent reviewer. The
final composed eight-file proposal is recorded in manifest
`c19dde52b40bc9a5bcb481f91c665394a831080c4494502a95a9dd6bf4dbbb3a`: core3 plus
that stage, with one trailing LF normalized in the owned module to match the actual
Git baseline. Compiler/native/PG execution of this stage remains NOT_RUN. Source
review and the pure arena regressions do not qualify the missing PSS/current-Content
owners, full runtime transition, process restart, or House activation.

The #2167 dependency head is 061c16a6f4ac5f337c56f1189421fa3ecb0cd127:
canonical encoder 9b15ae9969a65fd24f439aac30e415234a5a2f1a;
Foundation mount c00f1e391fc9f32d6e021f1bee2088d5452adec3;
Content re-export 054ad0d28e3c15051d692beb1d71068f09d8d17d;
native entry 091d086ae55dba495195d045f643a839349f2510;
House source 4a3b48e075efe2b2afd0323984aa72e57ce5bc5d.
These are actual upstream sources, not this PR's substitute implementation. Recompose
and recite exact post-merge preimages before any shared publication.
````

````markdown
# Independent source review — D1362 House claim proposal

## Scope and result

Reviewed the five-file inert proposal against main `f010472bcb6794a2cbc56dfaf755bd122c10225e`, including the subsequent historical decoder/replay and final replay-state correction. Exact current hashes are in `reviewed-manifest.json`; all five files matched the author's files at the final readback.

**Source review: PASS for this uninstalled preparation and history component after the replay-state correction.** This is not approval to activate its caller or publish held shared hunks. Native formatting, compilation, the six new predicate tests, the two new codec tests and PostgreSQL execution are **NOT_RUN** in this review.

## Finding corrected during review

The new history validator originally accepted `Committed` or `Released` even when a tag-3 claim receipt existed. Its matching live persist path requires `Released`, because entry-reservation consumption must precede the claim result while the destination is unbound at generation one. Accepting `Committed` in that final tag-3 validation weakened the same durable invariant.

The author corrected both checks: `record.state != Released` now refuses and the independent SQL requires `state = 3`. The preliminary lookup may still find a legacy `Committed` handoff; without a tag-3 receipt it returns no new claim result. Reviewed final handoff SHA256: `b6d305b753fdd27b9d0cf8fb9c56e227a0138031995c0363ee3147758c3874b9`.

## Checked source invariants

- The House claim constructor remains sealed through the actual owning-source interface. Historical evidence has no conversion into a prepared claim; the decoder constructs history data only.
- The predicate requires the complete independently current operation and both complete predecessor rows. Both holders move to the same destination session; the Character lease advances exactly once. The Platform security observation keeps its original age and content, with only its publication binding updated.
- Native preparation reads the locked handoff, original Channel and House assignment, node incarnation, exact source-session binding, current access revisions and the actual bounded interior loader in the supplied transaction. The native guard loader checks immutable publication history before successor construction.
- Preparation and persistence are tied to the same physical transaction by the private prepared value and `txid_current`. Persistence rechecks the complete guarded predecessors/history and the actual durable successor. It cannot itself supply actor-placement authority.
- The historical codec uses the existing bounded canonical envelope, preserves optional source fences and all ordered predecessor/successor data, verifies the supplied immutable initial receipt fields, bounds the tile before allocation and binds the destination scope to the native HouseId calculation. Replay compares the native handoff and all exact immutable guard-history rows. It neither re-prepares a live claim nor rolls current projections back to an older result.
- Six new predicate cases cover both successor holders/one lease advance, changed handoff/source bindings, complete same-revision predecessor changes, malformed successors/security refresh, security expiry and invalid entry shapes. Two new codec cases cover canonical round-trip, unknown/truncated/trailing data, a different initial receipt, native House identity and finite tile/envelope bounds. These are component test bodies, not executed native or PG evidence.

## Gates that remain concrete

1. **Cross-scope generation comparisons.** The current persisted-snapshot reconstruction and `foundation/admission_authority_publication.rs::validate_session_claims` compare a current scope generation with the initial fresh Channel's generation. They cannot use that ordering across independent scope identities. A valid Channel generation 5 → House generation 1 must be tested through successor reload and a subsequent lifecycle claim, such as TerminalRelease. Fixing only the reconciler would leave the latter validator refusing the House session. The author confirmed both locations are part of the separately held scope-proof delta.
2. **Actual House actor/reservation owner.** There is still no real owner that places the House actor and consumes the reservation before claims/bind/generation effects. The legacy terminal/generation-greater-than-one release path cannot establish the required unbound generation-one successor. Keep the new methods uncalled until the actual producer exists; durable row shape is not a substitute for placement authority.
3. **Publication order and tag.** Shared source stays after #2187. Codec/lifecycle tag 3 is explicitly provisional and must be checked against that actual predecessor before publication.
4. **Native qualification.** The complete handoff, history replay after restart, real same-transaction rollback, different-transaction prepared-value refusal, and a missing or mismatched immutable guard-history row need actual native PostgreSQL cases under the canonical owner harness. Component predicates and a codec cannot establish those results.

Final fresh-admission SHA256 reviewed: `c52be7d853c377ecb394b7d9240fa5418b17a4463c066865d62f5ec3ac0ed538`. The review changed no author files and launched no build or database process.

## Separate scope-generation proposal addendum

Also reviewed the unapplied `scope-generation-domain-proposal.patch`, SHA256 `abb944b2cf4688b70b38184e36f7085ebeb029d7f7ef9cebe47744f2624df450`, against the stated five-file component plus the actual `admission_recovery_inner.rs` baseline.

**Semantic source review: PASS as a separate held proposal.** Both minimum-generation comparisons now apply only when the current runtime scope equals the immutable initial Channel scope. Independent scope identities have independent ownership counters; their numeric ordering cannot validate a transition. Character/World, lease, connection, transport/state and complete predecessor checks remain in place. The change provides no proof that a different scope was actually assigned or an actor placed; those are still the actual native owner's obligations.

The two new component regressions address both paths: a distinct House generation one after initial Channel generation five can be reconstructed through the persisted-facts constructor and then enter TerminalRelease; the original Channel's generation-four rollback is still refused by both that constructor and the claim predicate. These tests construct in-memory fixture facts and call the real structural predicates. They perform no PostgreSQL persistence and establish no runtime House authority. The patch is unapplied and all its formatting, compilation and execution remain **NOT_RUN**.


## 2026-10-17 narrow session-use repair review

SOURCE REVIEW PASS for `deferred2165-session-use-repair.patch` SHA256 `266e97e1d506ea5a27377a3c03fd17e665614c9683fe472170d028b97ae9de58`, producing native `fresh_admission.rs` SHA256 `b49d1f243a53df2318215536b6f2afc36750564d97fa4b4527697fd4d9aff28b` from the previously reviewed `c52be7d8` file. Read the full changed prepare/persist/replay paths and existing `session_use_state`, `unused_session_revision`, and `commit_session_use` implementations. The private same-TX preparation now retains the actual unused revision; live persistence reloads the actual complete ledger and exact destination membership, requires one high-water advance and membership at that new revision, and binds character, destination session and nonzero native handoff operation bytes. History replay accepts the exact retained membership only beneath a complete nonregressed native ledger; it does not apply or grant a transition. The native session writer already commits that exact handoff ID as operation binding. No material finding in this delta. The added validator test covers the positive and independent negative predicates, including history after later advancement; it is pure data validation, not SQL/locking/native PostgreSQL qualification. No build or tests run by this reviewer.
````

````markdown
# Independent review: D1373 House carrier core

Current result: **SOURCE REVIEW PASS after narrow repair**. The three concrete lifecycle findings below are resolved in the exact second snapshot. The initial disposition was CHANGES REQUESTED and is retained here for traceability. No source was edited by this reviewer. Native formatting, compilation and the revised 14 pure cases are NOT_RUN here.

## Exact repaired snapshot and rereview

Second manifest SHA-256: `5b245a86ea51220c67159902b1ef6e6caaf46ce7c60ef3bef7ebbda92edd6bef`. Repaired actor placement SHA-256: `0e84f55e61c74335ef97bf730026c3220e67c06bf4a0bd0a7dcc451609bce59c`. Narrow repair patch SHA-256: `97bf39b2630648336f4d307c5544c5e264d18f502b100f97ccb211aa77d04875`. All four other source files are byte-identical to the first reviewed snapshot. Full exact copies are in `candidate2/`; the second manifest and narrow patch are retained beside this report.

The generation-exhaustion branch now performs the existing carrier's protected retirement and free-head advance before returning its typed refusal. Released reconciliation validates the retained source ActorRef through the real `validate_ref` before treating slot absence or recycling as idempotent completion. A separate reconciliation lookup requires an already-existing exact House/generation/node/revision namespace, cannot allocate or advance one, and permits cleanup without setting its active flag. The added retired-PREPARED regression confirms that cleanup does not reopen the old generation. `verify_consumed` now checks the retained node identity and registration revision, including separate negatives for each.

Three additional meaningful cases cover exhausted-head recovery, wrong-source Channel refusal and retired unresolved cleanup. The existing consumed-binding case gained the node negatives, bringing the selected source inventory to 14 pure cases. The former successful recycled-source and unknown-commit cases remain present. No material finding remains in this reviewed core; this does not extend the qualification boundary below to the forthcoming native consumer, active House authority or external PlayerSpellState transfer.

## Reviewed snapshot

The complete snapshot and hashes are in `reviewed-manifest.json` and `candidate/` beside this report. Initial actor placement source SHA-256: `1ad7bd6ebe561666a000bd53b58853eb11132786adcfc0c11484f2ca9b35a695`. Carrier SHA-256: `89f6584401ed55f4e0bbdcb4ced9df35bbd015d5aaa9182f3636d6ad51b3b426`.

Verified actual baseline Git blobs: carrier `52c22d810bac5bfe4173a4f13d9f0635ffd8ba1e`, foundation module `de761247000eaee0792f7d35dfe46a54406f57a1`, NPC actor `eea62e3949f2c79d78e5642f40df884d602b2bd8`. The handoff delta is relative to the previously reviewed D1362 source SHA-256 `b6d305b753fdd27b9d0cf8fb9c56e227a0138031995c0363ee3147758c3874b9`.

## Initial required repairs — resolved in the second snapshot

1. **Exhausted free-list head blocks all later House reservations.** `reserve_entry` returns from `generation.checked_add(1)` without retiring the exhausted slot or advancing `free_head`. The existing Channel `admit_inner` implements the protected generation-exhaustion behavior: change the selected slot to `Slot::Exhausted`, advance the free list, then return the typed exhaustion error. House must preserve that arena invariant. Required regression: exhaust the first reusable slot, receive the typed error, then reserve the next free slot without generation reuse.

2. **Released reconciliation accepts an unrelated source Channel as absence.** The initial code validates the supplied source runtime against its own continuity, then interprets `contains_committed_player == false` as evidence that the retained source was already removed. A different valid Channel produces false through WrongScope, leaving the real source untouched while marking the House slot Durable. Validate the retained source `ActorRef` against the supplied source's actual outer World/Channel/generation and slot index before interpreting current slot absence. The existing `validate_ref` provides that check without rejecting a safely recycled slot generation. Required regression: wrong source Channel refuses with both carriers unchanged, while existing same-source recycled-slot replay remains valid.

3. **Retirement prevents the promised reconciliation cleanup.** `retire` can mark a namespace inactive with an unresolved slot retained. `reconcile_entry_with_source` then calls `current_namespace`, which rejects that same inactive generation. A later generation cannot be constructed while the old occupied slot remains, leaving no cleanup route. Reconciliation needs a lookup of the already-existing exact namespace that cannot bootstrap or advance it, and can perform denial-only PREPARED rollback after local retirement under the exact old key/node/source/token proof. It must never reopen the namespace. Required regression: unresolved commit, retirement, actual Prepared reconciliation cleanup, and continued refusal to bootstrap the retired generation.

Additional accepted binding hardening: `verify_consumed` should compare the supplied native node ID and registration revision with the retained namespace. These fields are deliberately absent from the logical operation key; checking that key and the physical transaction alone does not itself perform this current namespace comparison. The author is adding the comparison and a changed-node regression. This suggestion does not assert that the unimplemented native authority producer already fabricates such a proof.

## Other source conclusions

The House slot uses the existing carrier arena, free list and occupied index. Its reserved tile remains present through Reserved, Materialized, Consumed, CommitInFlight and Durable, so consumption does not make the tile available to a competing reservation. Identity conflicts include the retained initial receipt, handoff, character, source lease/session, destination session, House/generation and tile.

The source hold obtains the actual committed Channel slot and lease, refuses an existing write fence or spell reservation, and installs an exact transition token. Materialization copies the real ActorState, full source VersionedPosition, lifecycle state and carrier combat state. The House destination stays the actual reservation tile; it does not relabel the Channel position. Before COMMIT initiation, Drop removes the exact staged House slot and lifts only its matching source token. After initiation, both owners are retained pending native outcome reconciliation. The forthcoming native consumer still needs to pair the two begin-commit operations correctly and provide actual commit/reconcile proof; these pure core methods alone do not qualify that integration.

The shared carrier delta preserves Channel references and total Channel APIs. Former Channel comparisons now explicitly select the Channel scope arm; the House variant refuses those APIs. The NPC change reads the same validated continuity ChannelId. The allocator extraction keeps the existing Channel continuity claim after successful allocation. No material unrelated Channel source change was found.

The HouseId move keeps one actual definition with private fields. The key grammar, World binding, scope-key bytes and instance hash domain/UUID shaping are unchanged. Durability retains the same error-bearing runtime_scope method and re-exports the single type; direct field reads become its existing accessors. Shared publication of this move and the NPC line remains subject to the parent's explicit CP allocation.

## Qualification limits

The source hold does not transfer the external `PlayerSpellState` owned by `ChannelSpellStates`; see the separate `spell-transfer/FINDING.md`. The author explicitly keeps the consumer staged and unbound. Node directory ownership, actual native PREPARED/RELEASED authority, full spell/item-use/timer state transfer, FND binding and live House dispatch must be qualified through their real owners. A local payload or retained receipt cannot replace those producers.

No native test, PostgreSQL test, public admission grant, runtime activation or GitHub write was performed by this reviewer. Source rereview of the exact repaired core is complete; execution and the separate native consumer remain outside this PASS.
````

````markdown
# Narrow independent source review — retained source payload before Released removal

**SOURCE REVIEW PASS.** No material finding in the narrow core3 delta. This is source inspection only; no compiler, native execution or tests were run by this reviewer.
````

````markdown
The eight-file supplied snapshot includes the previously reviewed core2 actor source `0e84f55e…`. Core3 actor `de655311…` and its narrow retained-source-payload repair were subsequently reviewed separately. They are source-compatible, but this report does not relabel the original manifest as a newly built combined packet. Root must record the actual combined source inventory for any native run.

Read-only copies, the exact supplied patch and the dependency reads are retained in this directory. No peer file was changed.

## Native ownership and transaction boundary

`PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims` retains private fields and is still constructed only by the actual native FreshAdmissionStore preparation. That preparation reads and locks the actual PREPARED handoff, source session, current Node/House/Channel assignment, access and guard predecessors, and loads the original #2163 HouseInteriorSnapshot. The stage does not construct one from a receipt, DTO, Boolean or expected fixture state. The new trait implementation exposes those retained native facts and the actual initial receipt key; it creates no active Content or transport authority.

The stage checks its supplied transaction's real `txid_current()` against the preparation's original physical transaction identity before using it. It consumes the preparation and retains an exclusive borrow of that SQL transaction, the actual Channel owner and the actual House directory through the lifetime of `StagedHouseEntry`. The caller therefore cannot commit the SQL transaction or independently mutate either borrowed physical owner while the stage exists. The stage has no bind or commit API. Dropping it releases the exact House reservation and source write fence through the existing RAII owners; no partial persistent successor is written here.

The source checks use the actual native current session snapshot and the actual ChannelRuntimeV1 owner: Active state, live transport, no control loss, identical session and character lease, matching World/Channel/scope generation, the real bound attacker lease, and the real positioned player under the fixed runtime movement context. The installed 0003 assignment guard requires every successor to advance ownership generation; the current native assignment held by preparation and the immutable runtime binding therefore share the same generation domain. No synthetic ChannelId or second player map is introduced.

## Actual Content and interior inputs

I independently fetched the agreed #2167 source at `061c16a6`: native entry blob `091d086ae55dba495195d045f643a839349f2510`, Content re-export `054ad0d28e3c15051d692beb1d71068f09d8d17d`, and canonical spatial encoder `9b15ae9969a65fd24f439aac30e415234a5a2f1a`. The called room/pin methods and canonical encoding exist with the expected types and visibility. The dependency is real source, not a fixture facade. The pending read accessors expose only the already-qualified finite House cells and the existing canonical single-House placement digest.

The proposed destination is canonically encoded and compared with the locked PREPARED bytes. Its qualified physical House membership must match the real target HouseId. The source room's World, server/client artifact digests, coordinate frame and map revision must match the actual runtime pin. The stage does not substitute an exterior fallback entry cell for an interior reservation.

The stage reads the installed 0047 `game_world_house_instances` identity joined to actual property ownership under share locks, including exact World/House/Instance, House revision, compatible Content digest and the same single-House placement digest. Missing or incompatible data refuses before acquiring a physical source hold. An Unknown catalogue also refuses; no inferred empty catalogue or entry-only fallback is installed.

The original native HouseInteriorSnapshot is retained without reconstruction. Its exact House and scope ownership generation, item bound and every canonical native item position are checked against the finite qualified House cell set. This remains a point-in-time loaded snapshot. It is neither a future permission nor proof of later content/occupancy currentness, which the code correctly does not claim.

## Materialization and remaining gates

After the native, spatial and loaded-source checks, the actual sequence is source hold, House reservation, materialization from the complete real source Slot payload, reservation consumption and exact consumed-token verification. Failure unwinds those physical reservations. `verify_consumed_in_owning_transaction` rechecks the original physical SQL transaction and exact reservation binding without writing a receipt or successor.

The actual external `ChannelSpellStates::actors` PlayerSpellState owner has not been transferred. The code deliberately provides no bind/commit method and claims no complete player transfer. Active full-bundle Content/currentness, the final FND/ingress path and the ordered owner integration remain held. No live owner is recovered from historical receipt bytes.

The source boundary and Rust visibility/lifetime design appear consistent on inspection. **Compilation and all native/PG execution remain NOT_RUN for this stage snapshot.** Source review does not replace the pending shared-file scope grants, actual dependency merges or Root's native source-assembly verification under D1329 and the sequential PC build policy.
````

````diff
+    #[test]
+    fn house_actor_placement_npc_paths_preserve_validated_channel_identity() {
+        use super::super::npc_actor::{
+            NpcPlacement, NpcPlacementError, NpcPlacementTable, NpcPlacements,
+        };
+        use super::super::{ChannelContentPin, ChannelId, MovementFacing};
+
+        fn runtime(seed: u8, capacity: usize) -> ChannelRuntimeV1 {
+            let world = WorldId::decode(&uuid(1)).expect("fixture World");
+            ChannelRuntimeV1::from_committed_assignment(
+                world,
+                ChannelId::decode(&uuid(seed)).expect("fixture Channel"),
+                NodeId::decode(&uuid(3)).expect("fixture Node"),
+                7,
+                5,
+                11,
+                "runtime-scope-assignment:11",
+                capacity,
+                ChannelContentPin::test(world),
+            )
+            .expect("actual Channel runtime under a pure fixture assignment")
+        }
+
+        fn assert_npcs(
+            runtime: &ChannelRuntimeV1,
+            expected_channel: ChannelId,
+            actors: &[ExactActorRef],
+            keys: &[&str],
+        ) {
+            // The enum's Channel payload is precisely the old carrier.channel_id.
+            // Compare that stored value independently of the continuity-based NPC write.
+            let CarrierScopeDomain::Channel(stored_channel) = &runtime.carrier.scope_domain else {
+                panic!("NPCs require the actual Channel carrier domain");
+            };
+            assert_eq!(*stored_channel, expected_channel);
+            assert_eq!(runtime.continuity.channel_id, *stored_channel);
+            assert_eq!(
+                runtime.carrier.validate_current_continuity(&runtime.continuity),
+                Ok(())
+            );
+            assert_eq!(actors.len(), keys.len());
+            for (actor, key) in actors.iter().zip(keys) {
+                assert_eq!(actor.channel_id(), *stored_channel);
+                assert_eq!(actor.world_id(), runtime.carrier.world_id);
+                assert_eq!(actor.scope_generation(), runtime.carrier.scope_generation);
+                assert_eq!(runtime.npc_key(*actor), Some(*key));
+                let index = usize::try_from(actor.actor_local_id() - 1).expect("NPC slot index");
+                let Slot::NpcOccupied { position, .. } = &runtime.carrier.slots[index] else {
+                    panic!("the returned NPC is in the original physical arena");
+                };
+                assert_eq!(position.context.channel_id, *stored_channel);
+                let mut wrong_channel = *actor;
+                wrong_channel.0.channel_id = ChannelId::decode(&uuid(222)).expect("other Channel");
+                assert_eq!(runtime.npc_key(wrong_channel), None);
+            }
+            assert_eq!(
+                runtime.visible_entities().npcs.iter().map(|npc| npc.actor).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
+                actors
+            );
+        }
+
+        fn assert_wrong_scope_is_inert(runtime: &mut ChannelRuntimeV1, table: &NpcPlacementTable) {
+            for production in [false, true] {
+                let before = runtime.carrier.clone();
+                let catalogue_called = std::cell::Cell::new(false);
+                assert_eq!(
+                    runtime.place_npcs(table, |_| {
+                        catalogue_called.set(true);
+                        true
+                    }, production),
+                    Err(NpcPlacementError::Carrier(CarrierError::WrongScope))
+                );
+                assert!(!catalogue_called.get(), "continuity is checked before NPC eligibility");
+                assert_eq!(runtime.carrier, before);
+            }
+        }
+
+        let placement = |x| NpcPlacement {
+            floor: -2,
+            y: 7,
+            x,
+            direction: MovementFacing::South,
+        };
+        let table = NpcPlacementTable {
+            npcs: vec![
+                NpcPlacements {
+                    key: "oteryn:npc.a".into(),
+                    placements: vec![placement(4), placement(5)],
+                },
+                NpcPlacements {
+                    key: "oteryn:npc.b".into(),
+                    placements: vec![placement(6)],
+                },
+            ],
+        };
+        let empty = NpcPlacementTable::default();
+        for seed in [2, 19, 99] {
+            let channel = ChannelId::decode(&uuid(seed)).expect("expected stored Channel");
+            for production in [false, true] {
+                let mut current = runtime(seed, 4);
+                let outcome = current.place_npcs(&table, |_| true, production).expect("NPC placement");
+                assert!(outcome.not_admitted.is_empty());
+                assert_npcs(&current, channel, &outcome.actors, &["oteryn:npc.a", "oteryn:npc.a", "oteryn:npc.b"]);
+                let before = current.carrier.clone();
+                assert_eq!(current.place_npcs(&table, |_| true, production), Err(NpcPlacementError::AlreadyPlaced));
+                assert_eq!(current.carrier, before);
+            }
+
+            // Production refusal cannot leak the admitted prefix. The same unchanged
+            // owner can then take the non-production path, which places only that prefix.
+            let mut current = runtime(seed, 4);
+            let before = current.carrier.clone();
+            assert_eq!(
+                current.place_npcs(&table, |key| key == "oteryn:npc.a", true),
+                Err(NpcPlacementError::NpcNotAdmitted("oteryn:npc.b".into()))
+            );
+            assert_eq!(current.carrier, before);
+            let outcome = current.place_npcs(&table, |key| key == "oteryn:npc.a", false).expect("eligible NPC prefix");
+            assert_eq!(outcome.not_admitted, vec!["oteryn:npc.b"]);
+            assert_npcs(&current, channel, &outcome.actors, &["oteryn:npc.a", "oteryn:npc.a"]);
+
+            let mut current = runtime(seed, 4);
+            let before = current.carrier.clone();
+            let outcome = current.place_npcs(&table, |_| false, false).expect("all held outside production");
+            assert_eq!(outcome.not_admitted, vec!["oteryn:npc.a", "oteryn:npc.b"]);
+            assert_npcs(&current, channel, &outcome.actors, &[]);
+            assert_eq!(current.carrier, before);
+            for production in [false, true] {
+                let outcome = current.place_npcs(&empty, |_| true, production).expect("empty NPC table");
+                assert_npcs(&current, channel, &outcome.actors, &[]);
+                assert_eq!(current.carrier, before);
+            }
+
+            let mut current = runtime(seed, 2);
+            let before = current.carrier.clone();
+            assert_eq!(current.place_npcs(&table, |_| true, true), Err(NpcPlacementError::Carrier(CarrierError::CapacityExceeded)));
+            assert_eq!(current.carrier, before);
+
+            // The first NPC is physically staged before the second slot exhausts.
+            // Rollback must restore that complete original Channel arena.
+            let mut current = runtime(seed, 4);
+            let Slot::VacantReusable { generation, .. } = &mut current.carrier.slots[1] else {
+                panic!("second reusable NPC slot");
+            };
+            *generation = u64::MAX;
+            let before = current.carrier.clone();
+            assert_eq!(current.place_npcs(&table, |_| true, true), Err(NpcPlacementError::Carrier(CarrierError::ActorGenerationExhausted)));
+            assert_eq!(current.carrier, before);
+
+            for invalid in 0..4 {
+                let mut current = runtime(seed, 4);
+                match invalid {
+                    0 => current.continuity.channel_id = ChannelId::decode(&uuid(222)).expect("wrong Channel"),
+                    1 => current.continuity.world_id = WorldId::decode(&uuid(223)).expect("wrong World"),
+                    2 => current.continuity.current_generation = ScopeOwnershipGeneration::new(6).expect("different generation"),
+                    _ => current.retire_owner_cycle(),
+                }
+                assert_wrong_scope_is_inert(&mut current, &table);
+                assert_wrong_scope_is_inert(&mut current, &empty);
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A real House arena must never acquire a Channel identity via the NPC path.
+        // This deliberately invalid composition is confined to the pure negative fixture.
+        let a = authority(10);
+        let mut directory = HouseActorDirectory::with_explicit_bound(1).expect("House directory");
+        drop(directory.reserve_entry(&a).expect("actual House namespace, then rollback"));
+        let mut wrong_domain = runtime(2, 4);
+        wrong_domain.carrier = directory.namespaces[0].carrier.take().expect("actual House carrier");
+        assert_wrong_scope_is_inert(&mut wrong_domain, &table);
+        assert_wrong_scope_is_inert(&mut wrong_domain, &empty);
+        let before = wrong_domain.carrier.clone();
+        let visible = wrong_domain.visible_entities();
+        assert!(visible.players.is_empty() && visible.creatures.is_empty() && visible.npcs.is_empty());
+        assert_eq!(wrong_domain.current_live_creature_candidates(4), Err(CarrierError::WrongScope));
+        assert_eq!(wrong_domain.carrier, before);
+    }
+}
````

### Artifact `actor2165-npc-review/REVIEW.md`

Original bytes: 8127; SHA-256 `b4e95ff563b2f269b82746a8dfdd0b93625861c19261cd02b2efcdfec3f70efe`; encoding `utf8`.

<!-- BEGIN ARTIFACT actor2165-npc-review/REVIEW.md utf8 b4e95ff563b2f269b82746a8dfdd0b93625861c19261cd02b2efcdfec3f70efe 8127 -->
````markdown
# Independent source review: D1401b NPC successor and Channel field closure

Result: **SOURCE REVIEW PASS for this narrow successor. No material finding.**

Reviewer: `/root/pr_2145_2147_2148`; author: `/root/pr_2165_2166`.
Review date: 2026-10-17. This was a read-only review of the author's frozen
files and actual pinned dependencies. The reviewer did not modify the source,
run rustfmt, compile Rust, execute the 16 cases, use RDC, or run PostgreSQL.
Native formatting, compilation, and the successor's test execution remain
**NOT_RUN**. This result does not grant shared-source publication, activate the
House consumer, or qualify the entire earlier stage assembly.

## Exact reviewed packet

The packet is `actor2165-npc-regression-next/`, with the following full files:

| Path under the packet | Reviewed SHA-256 | Bytes |
| --- | --- | ---: |
| `apps/game-server/src/house_scope/actor_placement.rs` | `deec44ffb937e6371335c8501637c2add075ce1d72151ec074a2dfb886dd406c` | 68,598 |
| `apps/game-server/src/foundation/runtime_actor_carrier.rs` | `067838d43bd1006909a6783d0b4f5493dfd64e52fc49d9e03b6e769457406652` | 644,114 |

The reviewed patch is `actor2165-npc-successor.patch`, SHA-256
`e8eda1d3062c853a0027d914c4a3fc0bf247bdfccd14732596c78686d01171d9`.
Its manifest is `actor2165-npc-successor-manifest.json`, SHA-256
`aed49f44f70c368adae97f0b518e56808a276e6f37c4ca5b1067116d2394e277`.

The exact preimages are actor placement
`de655311758bdc85a3bb67a529467d250add3039df8b328d9475b35cda8020b4`
and carrier
`89f6584401ed55f4e0bbdcb4ced9df35bbd015d5aaa9182f3636d6ad51b3b426`.
An independently generated unified diff from both complete preimages to both
complete successors equals the entire proposed patch byte for byte. Thus this
review's narrow delta contains the stated one added test and seven repaired
reads, without an additional hidden source change. Independently removing the
new test restores the complete previous actor source modulo its terminal line
ending; all 15 earlier cases and production code are retained byte for byte.

## Seven reads and Channel equivalence

The original carrier preimage contained seven accesses to the removed
`ChannelActorCarrier.channel_id` field: six in production code and one in an
existing test fixture. No direct read of that removed carrier field remains
in the reviewed successor.

Four production conversions use `self.continuity.channel_id` only after an
existing native guard has established equality with the actual stored
`CarrierScopeDomain::Channel` payload:

| Owner path | Existing guard before the changed projection |
| --- | --- |
| `commit_welter_consume_inner` | `validate_ref` for the actual caster |
| `smelly_unambiguous_targets` | `validate_ref` for the actual caster |
| `commit_boreth_death` | `creature_target_identity` and `projected_death`, both calling `validate_ref` |
| `commit_rum_barrel_death` | `creature_target_identity` and `projected_death`, both calling `validate_ref` |

`validate_ref` first calls `validate_current_continuity`. That check requires
an active guard, the same World, the same actual Channel domain and identifier,
and the same scope generation. It then checks the ActorRef against both the
guard and carrier. No relevant callback mutates the scope domain or continuity
between that validation and the replacement read. Therefore each replacement
retains the old Channel value on a successful native path and still refuses
House or mismatched continuity.

The two read-only census methods did not previously validate current
continuity. Their replacements correctly project the stored enum payload
directly: `visible_entities` returns the default empty projection for a House
domain, while `current_live_creature_candidates` returns `WrongScope`. For a
Channel domain both still use the old stored identifier, including the
existing Channel retirement behavior. The change does not silently add a new
continuity rule to those Channel census paths.

The seventh replacement is in the existing Welter negative fixture's
continuity advance. It preserves that fixture's actual Channel identity; it
does not mint or convert a House identifier. The existing one-line
`npc_actor.rs` conversion is unchanged by this successor and is exercised by
the added test.

## Actual sibling-module audit

The audit used the actual dependency source at
`Oteryn/Oteryn-Game@f010472bcb6794a2cbc56dfaf755bd122c10225e`.
Twenty-four explicit child/path dependencies were fetched and read, including
the nested spell/conditions/player-death tests. The exact Git blob identities
and every remaining `.channel_id` hit are retained in adjacent `inventory.json`,
SHA-256 `5bc4ceac177feabf17d23a7806e7b6b705e8106b5af2fe54cc3d9f1d577ad39a`.

No additional reference to the removed carrier field was found in these 24
actual dependency files. Their remaining `channel_id` reads belong to live
ActorRef, runtime binding, continuity/owner-grant fixtures, or methods on those
types; those fields were not removed. The candidate NPC child and the owned
actor-placement child were checked separately. This is a bounded source
closure audit, not an assertion that arbitrary future main changes or the
entire uncompiled stage have no compiler errors. GitHub search alone was not
used as negative proof.

## New regression and actual owner behavior

The one new case is
`house_actor_placement_npc_paths_preserve_validated_channel_identity`.
There are 16 `#[test]` cases in the complete owned actor-placement source.
The new case exercises the actual `ChannelRuntimeV1::place_npcs` owner, its
physical `Slot::NpcOccupied` arena, `npc_key`, visible projection, and native
failure rollback. It does not substitute a detached NPC allocator.

The case compares the actual stored enum Channel payload independently with
the continuity value used in the NPC write. For three distinct Channels it
checks both production and non-production admission, multiple placements,
returned ActorRefs, physical position context, stored NPC keys, and refusal
of an ActorRef with a different Channel identifier.

Meaningful refusal and atomicity branches are included: duplicate placement;
production with a held NPC after an eligible prefix; non-production with only
the eligible prefix; all-held and empty tables; insufficient capacity; and a
second free-list slot at `u64::MAX` after the first NPC has been staged. The
last case reaches actual `admit_npc` generation exhaustion and compares the
complete carrier before and after the existing owner restores slots,
free-head, and occupied index.

Wrong Channel, wrong World, wrong scope generation, and retirement are tested
with both populated and empty tables in both admission modes. The eligibility
closure must remain uncalled, so the guard cannot be accidentally deferred
until allocation. The actual NPC implementation checks current continuity
before eligibility; its later `admit_npc` uses that validated continuity.

A House arena produced through the real reservation owner is inserted into
a Channel runtime only inside the deliberate pure negative fixture. NPC
placement refuses that composition before eligibility, both census methods
refuse to expose a Channel projection, and the full arena stays unchanged.
No fake ChannelId is constructed from House identity. This negative fixture
does not claim production House activation, player-spell-state transfer, or
an active Content/admission producer.

The test's imports, actual constructor signature, private-child accessibility,
and return/error comparisons are consistent with the inspected source. This
is source reasoning; the compiler and actual test runner remain the required
next qualification steps in an authorized House build window.

## Qualification and ownership boundary

The prior 15-case core and the native stage remain separate frozen evidence.
The successful earlier 11-case claims run does not validate this successor.
No test PASS is claimed here. Shared carrier edits retain their CP ownership
and order requirements, and this review does not broaden the separately
granted NPC field hunk into permission for the full actor or PSS integration.
````

### Artifact `actor2165-npc-successor-notes.md`

Original bytes: 5189; SHA-256 `4d8b5a61d0dced245186eab10acea1fa56747e7a52b2dee398f75bb7f8449b61`; encoding `utf8`.

<!-- BEGIN ARTIFACT actor2165-npc-successor-notes.md utf8 4d8b5a61d0dced245186eab10acea1fa56747e7a52b2dee398f75bb7f8449b61 5189 -->
````markdown
# #2165 D1401b — separate carrier and NPC regression successor

This candidate changes two complete source files relative to the preserved
`actor2165-stage-native` assembly. It does not alter that frozen assembly or the
published checkpoint at `291bd9dc748707b29e65ce7b51e40151ab98157f`.

## Why a successor is needed

The private carrier's field became `CarrierScopeDomain::Channel(ChannelId) |
House(HouseId)` in the D1373 proposal. Seven direct reads of the old
`ChannelActorCarrier.channel_id` field remained in the carrier source. This is a
source-level compilation defect discovered before the first native build of the
stage. It is not a failed native test result. The existing eleven passing pure
D1362 claim tests belong to their separate, exact six-file assembly.

The complete source patch is `actor2165-npc-successor.patch`. The two full files
are in `actor2165-npc-regression-next/`; the JSON payload contains those same bytes.

## Seven bounded read repairs

| Existing function or fixture | Replacement and preserved condition |
| --- | --- |
| `visible_entities` | Read the stored `Channel(id)` payload directly. A House carrier returns an empty value snapshot. Existing Channel fields, context filtering and census order remain unchanged. |
| `commit_welter_consume_inner` | Use `self.continuity.channel_id` after the existing `validate_ref` proves exact World, Channel and generation. |
| `smelly_unambiguous_targets` | Use that same validated continuity after the existing caster reference and position checks. |
| `welter_native_source_owner_proof` | The pure fixture advances the real owner continuity using its own Channel ID. |
| `commit_boreth_death` | Use validated continuity after the existing current-owner identity and projected-death proof. |
| `commit_rum_barrel_death` | Use validated continuity after the same existing current-owner and projected-death boundary. |
| `current_live_creature_candidates` | Read the stored `Channel(id)` payload directly. House returns `WrongScope`; all existing Channel census behavior remains unchanged. |

No new public helper, alias Channel identity, default actor state, extra carrier,
scope grant or runtime activation is introduced. The single NPC field change
already proposed under D1401b remains byte-identical in its separate source file.

## Actual NPC regression

The new owned case is
`house_actor_placement_tests::house_actor_placement_npc_paths_preserve_validated_channel_identity`.
It calls the existing `ChannelRuntimeV1::place_npcs`, `npc_key` and
`visible_entities` implementations on actual carrier slots, using explicitly pure
assignment/Content fixtures. The oracle compares the enum's stored Channel payload
(the former field) with validated continuity, returned actor identities, stored
position contexts and the visible NPC census.

It uses three distinct Channel IDs and exercises both production and non-production
placement, refused and partially eligible catalogues, all-held and empty tables,
already-placed refusal, capacity refusal, and a real partial-allocation rollback
when the second slot's generation is exhausted. Negative fixtures cover mismatched
Channel, World and generation, owner retirement, and the actual House arena. The
wrong-scope cases also require refusal before the eligibility callback and preserve
the complete carrier footprint. The House case verifies that the two newly explicit
Channel projections cannot produce House-as-Channel results.

All fifteen existing owned test bodies are byte-identical. The resulting filter
`house_actor_placement_tests::` contains sixteen pure test functions. This is a
source inventory, not an execution result.

## Verification and remaining gates

Local verification checked all sixteen original assembly hashes, the exact original
fifteen test bodies, whitespace in the added deltas, and application of the patch to
copies of both preimages. Patch reconstruction produced the full candidate bytes.
Independent **SOURCE REVIEW PASS** is retained in `actor2165-npc-review/REVIEW.md`,
SHA256 `b4e95ff563b2f269b82746a8dfdd0b93625861c19261cd02b2efcdfec3f70efe`.
The reviewer also read twenty-four actual pinned child modules and found no further
access to the removed carrier field. Their Git blob inventory is retained in
`actor2165-npc-review/inventory.json`, SHA256
`5bc4ceac177feabf17d23a7806e7b6b705e8106b5af2fe54cc3d9f1d577ad39a`.

Native formatting, compilation, LIST and execution of this successor are **NOT_RUN**.
No House heavy-build window after MAIL/HUNT has been granted. No PostgreSQL command
was executed. The published evidence remains unchanged and does not inherit results
for this new source.

D1401a/c/d/e remain held exactly as decided in
<https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2131#issuecomment-6237066255>.
The latest direct read still reports #2167 and #2132 open and unmerged. The additional
crate-private Foundation helper re-exports in the stage are not covered by D1401a's
identity-and-three-constants wording and require a precise follow-up allocation.
The actual spell owner move/rollback/adopt port remains deferred. Production entry
and login remain `HOUSE_CLOSED`; exit remains `ExitNotAdmitted`.
````

### Artifact `actor2165-private-exports-clarification.md`

Original bytes: 4168; SHA-256 `98fe15c2ae224a5c751faa0aae7bfc1501b8d02bdb64c780534b5e39e69ff623`; encoding `utf8`.

<!-- BEGIN ARTIFACT actor2165-private-exports-clarification.md utf8 98fe15c2ae224a5c751faa0aae7bfc1501b8d02bdb64c780534b5e39e69ff623 4168 -->
````markdown
## #2165 — precise additional private seam after D1401a

Prepared for root's next concrete CP follow-up. This is an unapplied source proposal;
it grants no permission and changes none of the frozen assembly files.

D1401a orders the same `HouseId` and its three constants after #2167 and #2132 merge.
Its wording permits only those identity exports. The actual retained native stage
also needs the following six crate-private names to connect the existing Durability
prepare object and the owned physical consumer without making the carrier public.

| Additional crate-private name | Existing concrete consumer in the retained stage |
| --- | --- |
| `HouseActorAuthorityV1` | The native `PreparedHouseScopeTransitionClaims` implementation in `durability/fresh_admission.rs`; authority method calls in `house_scope/mod.rs`. |
| `house_actor_authority_seal` | The sealed implementation on that actual native prepare object. Its constructor remains Durability-private. |
| `HouseActorDirectory` | The borrowed physical House owner supplied to `stage_house_entry_in_transaction`. |
| `HouseEntryPlacement` | The retained real reservation/materialization/consumption guard in `StagedHouseEntry`. |
| `HouseSourceActorHold` | The borrowed original physical Channel slot retained by the same staged operation. |
| `HousePlacementError` | The existing error carried by `HouseEntryStageError`. |

The earlier full proposal also exported `HouseActorReconcileAuthorityV1`,
`HouseActorReconcileOutcome`, `HouseActorSettledState` and `HousePlacedActor`. No
external caller in the current native stage needs those four names. This narrower
request leaves them inside the private carrier subtree until their actual consumer
is separately reviewable. It is a reduction of the earlier proposal, not a visibility
increase.

**Question for CP:** may these exact six crate-private exports be allocated to #2165
in the later held native-stage integration slot, separately from D1401a's public
identity exports? We preserve the prerequisite that #2167 and #2132 both merge,
D1401c's later #2187 gate, and the remaining D1373/SESSION-INGRESS-1 order. The exact
post-merge Foundation blob will be re-cited before application. The carrier module
stays private; this block adds no constructor, bind/commit method, admission grant or
PlayerSpellState access. The existing staged consumer still cannot commit the SQL
transaction or attach gameplay.

The direct read performed for this follow-up reports #2167 open and unmerged at
`061c16a6f4ac5f337c56f1189421fa3ecb0cd127`, and #2132 open and unmerged at
`e4adb97e1cfb68b7fba5cd024fa34531ab1e117b`. Those observations do not satisfy the
merge gates. The later spell-owner request still must identify the pending item-use,
training, familiar and timer owners; none is silently covered by this export block.

### Concrete current preimage and unapplied hunk

Current reviewed Foundation preimage Git blob:
`de761247000eaee0792f7d35dfe46a54406f57a1`.
Preimage SHA256:
`abc9bd0885bd624520d3a31a62c52604138f6dfeb8046a643896cf5e89f21691`.
The exact 744-byte patch is `actor2165-minimal-private-exports-proposal.patch`, SHA256
`a1321782949cb79ca57875a61526d1f040b11c4df0fd88041675c7776713cd0e`.
Its complete resulting-source SHA256 would be
`2528e305a7b0ffa0cb30b2760b867b21848769094b3aa8e7c1658426775b13a6`.
This is a concrete present-source proposal, not a claim about a future merged blob.

```diff
--- a/apps/game-server/src/foundation/mod.rs
+++ b/apps/game-server/src/foundation/mod.rs
@@ -21,6 +21,14 @@
 mod protocol;
 #[allow(dead_code)]
 mod runtime_actor_carrier;
+// D1373: the one native House identity and the one existing carrier's House arm.
+pub use runtime_actor_carrier::house_actor_placement::{
+    HOUSE_KEY_MAX_BYTES, HOUSE_SCOPE_CHARACTERS_MAX, HOUSE_TILE_MAX_BYTES, HouseId,
+};
+pub(crate) use runtime_actor_carrier::house_actor_placement::{
+    HouseActorAuthorityV1, HouseActorDirectory, HouseEntryPlacement, HousePlacementError,
+    HouseSourceActorHold, house_actor_authority_seal,
+};
 pub(crate) use runtime_actor_carrier::npc_actor::{
     NpcPlacement, NpcPlacementError, NpcPlacementTable, NpcPlacements,
 };
```
````
